20a8(phal chen): ji snyed pa de bzhin gshegs pa de dag gis mngon par thugasu chud ba de dag tu/sangs rgyas bcomaldan 'das gang ji snyed mya ngan las 'das pa de dag gi dge ba'i rtsa ba ji lta ba bzhin du byang chub sems dpa' yid cheso//de bzhin 
20b1(phal chen): gshegs pa ma byon pa de dang /de'i dus su gtogs pa phyi ma'i mtha'i mur bstan pa/dpag tu med pa mtha' dang dbus med pa shin tu bstan pa dag tu/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das gang ji snyed 'byung bar 'gyur ba de dag gi dge 
20b2(phal chen): ba'i rtsa ba la yang byang chub sems dpa' de yid cheso//da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi ye shes mtha' dang dbus med par bstan pa ma lus par mngon par sangs rgyas pa'i/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'angs de dag
20b3(phal chen): gi dge ba'i rtsa ba la yang /byang chub sems dpa' de yi ches shing yongasu 'dzin par byed/yongasu rgyas par byed/rab tu 'thob par byed/khong du chud par byed/rab tu reg par byed/shin tu sgrub par byed/rgyud gcig
20b4(phal chen): tu gyur par byed/sangs rgyas thams cad kyang mnyam pa nyidu khong du chud par byedo//de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar sems bskyed pa de ni de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi gdung rgyud mi 'chad pa'i phyir 
20b5(phal chen): skye'o//'jig rten gyi khams ma lus pa thams cad rgyas par dgang ba'i phyir skye'o//'jig rten gyi kham sa thams cad gtogs pa'i sems can yongasu bsgral ba'i phyir skye'o//'jig rtan gyi 
20b6(phal chen): khams thams cad 'du ba dang 'jig pa yongasu shes par bya ba'i phyir skye'o//'jig rton gyi khams thams cad gtogs pa'i soms can rnams kyi kun nas nyon mongs pa dang /rnam par byang ba yongasu shes 
20b7(phal chen): par bya ba'i phyir skye'o//'jig rten gyi khams thaMd kyi srid pa gsum yongasu dag pa yongasu shes par bya ba yi phyir skye'o//sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa dang /bag la nyal ba dang /bag chags thams cad 
20b8(phal chen): yongasu shes par bya ba'i phyir/sems can thams cad kyi shi 'pho dang skye ba yongasu shes par bya ba'i spyir skye'o//sems can tham sa cad kyi dbang po dang sbyor ba yongasu shes par bya ba' phyir skye'o//sems can
21a1(phal chen): @#//thams cad kyi sems dang spyod pa yongasu shes par bya ba'i phyir skye'o//sems can thams cad kyi dus gsum gyi shes pa yongasu shes par bya ba'i+i phyir skye ste/sems bskyed pa nges/
21a2(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'das pa dag dang nye bar 'gyuro//sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dasam byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba rnams dang nye bar 'gyuro//'das ba'i sangs rgyas kyi byang chub tu nye bar 'gro'o//ma byon pa 
21a3(phal chen): dang da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas kyi byang chug tu nye bar 'gro'o//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas 'das pa rnams kyi chos kyang thob par 'gyuro//sangs rgyas bcam ldan 'das ma byon pa rnams kyi chos kyang thobapar 'gyuro//
21a4(phal chen): sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'a das da ltar byung ba rnams kyi chos kyang thob bar 'gyuro//sangs rgyas pa bcom ldan 'das 'das rnams dang mnyam pa nyidu nye bar 'gro'o//sangs rgas bcom ldan 'das ma byon pa
21a5(phal chen): dang /da ltar byung ba rnam sa dang mnyam pa nyidu nye ba 'gro'o//sangs rgyas bcam ldana'adas 'das pa rnam sa kyi ye shes kyi chog sa rnam par dag par 'gyuro//sangs rgyas ma byon pa
21a6(phal chen): byon pa dang /da ltar byu ba rnams kyi ye shes kyi tshogs kyang rnam par dag par 'gyuro //sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das 'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i stobs la thub pa med pa yang shin tu brtan 
21a7(phal chen): par 'gyuro//'das pa dang /ma byon da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'dad dag gi mi bsnyengs pa yongasu dag pa'ang yongasu rdzogs par byedo//sangs rgyas bcom lda 'das 'das pa rnams kyi 
21a8(phal chen): bstan par bya ba'i sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi rgyan mnyam pa nyid kyang rjesu 'thobo//sangs rgyas bcom ldana'adas ma byon pa dang /da ltar byang ba rnams kyi bstan par bya ba'i sangs rgas kyi chos kyi rgyan mnyamapa nyid kyang 
21b1(phal chen): yongasu rdzogs par byedo//namakha'i dbyings kyi mthar thug pa chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med par shin tu bstan pa'i dus gsum mnyam pa nyidu thugasu chud pa'i sangs rgyas kyi ye shes kyang rjesu 'thob po//de 
21b2(phal chen): ci'i phyir zhe na/de ltar de ni sangs rgyasu shog shig ces bya ba'i sems bskyed pa des/'das pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das rnams dang nye ba yin/ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 
21b3(phal chen): 'das rnams dang nye ba yin/'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung pa'i sangs rgyas kyi yul rnams dang mnyam pa yin/'das pa'i sangs rgyas kyi chos rnams dang mnyam pa yin no//ma 'ongs pa dang /da 
21b4(phal chen): ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas kyi chos rnam sa dang mnyam pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi spyod yul thams cad dang mnyam pa yin/gcig tu nges pa'i lus gcig tu gyur pas kyang /dus gsum du mnyam pa'i 
21b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas thams cad kyi ye shes dang 'grogs pa yang thob ste/ye shes de dang 'grogs par gyur pa'i sems bskyed par 'byung ba de skyes ma thag tu phyogs bcun/sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
21b6(phal chen): rnams kyang chedu brjod pa mdzado//sems bskyed pa de nyid rten du mdzad 'dul ba ji bzhin du/chos kyang yang dag par ston na/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad du yang 'dul ba tshad med pa rnams 'byung bar 'gyur/
21b7(phal chen): 'jig rten gyi khams thams chad kyang rab tu g.yo bar 'gyur/'jig rten gyi khams tha mas cad kyang snang bas rgyas par 'gyur/'jig rten gyi lams thams cad kyi ngan song yang rgyun chad par 'gyur/'jig
21b8(phal chen): rten gyi khams thams cad kyang yongasu dag par 'gyur/'jig rtenagyi khams thams cad sangs rgyas 'byung ba yang dag par ston par 'gyur/sems can thams cad kyang rab tu dga' bar 'gyur/chos kyi
22a1(phal chen): @#//dbyings thams cad la yang rab tu 'jug par 'gyur/sangs rgyas kyi rigs thams cad kyang yongs su 'dzin par 'gyur/sangs rgyas gyi rigs kyi snang ba thams cad kyang rab tu thob par 'gyur 
22a2(phal chen): te/dus gsum du gtogs pa las sems bskyed pa der/sangs rgyas sam/sangs rgyas kyi chos sam/byang chub sems dpa' 'am/byang chub sems dpa'i chos sam/rang sangs rgyas sam/rang sangs rgyas kyi chos
22a3(phal chen): sam/nyan thos sam/nyan thos kyi chos sam/'jig rten pa 'am/'jig rten pa'i chos sam/'jig rten las 'das pa 'am/'jig rten las 'das ba'i chos sam/sems can nam/sems gyi chos kyang rung 
22a4(phal chen): ste/ma 'das pa gang yang med de/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid ni ma gto/gso//de ltar na/sems bskyed pa der ni chos kyi dbyings thams cad 'dus so//de nas sangs rgyas kyi mthus/de'i tshe phyogs 
22a5(phal chen): bcur 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed rnam pa drug tu g.yos par gyur te/g.yos so//rab tu g.yos so//kun tu g.yos so//'gul to//rab tu 'gul 
22a6(phal chen): to ru ru dgu'o sdeg go/rab tu sde go/kun tu sde go/chem chem/rab tu chem chem/kun tu chem chemo//'ur 'ur/rab tu 'ur 'ur/kun tu 'ur 'uro//shig shig/rab tu shig shig/kun tu shig shig go/de nas sangs
22a7(phal chen): rgyas kyi mthu dang /sems bskyed pa de'i yon tan yang dag par brjod pa'i chos nyid rab tu thob pa'i phyir/lha rdzas kyi me tog gi char yang mngon par bab po/lha rdzas kyi spos kyi char yang mngon par bab bo/lha rdzas
22a8(phal chen): kyi phye ma'i char yang mngon par bab pa po//lha rdzas kyi phreng ba'i char yang mngon par babs po/lha rdzas kyi gos kyi char yang mngon par pab po/lha rdzas kyi rin po che'i char yang mngon par bab/lha rdzas kyi rgyan gyi 
22b1(phal chen): char yang mngon par bab po/lha rdzas kyi rol mo rnams kyang mngon par sgra 'byin par gyur to//lha rdzas kyi snang ba yang rab tu byungo//lha rdzas kyi 'od kyang rab tu byungo//phyogs bcur 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi
22b2(phal chen): zhing gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 'das pa'i pha rol nas/de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi blo grosu mtshan btags pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdul shin tu phra pa snyed kyis zhal mngon par bstan te/gsung brjod pa chos kyi
22b3(phal chen): blo gros legaso legaso//phyogs bcu na de bzhin gshegs pa sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rdulashin tu phra ba snyed kyi chos bstan pa de la chedu brjod pa mdzado//nged kyis kyang chos bstan pa 'di nyid shin tu rnam par phye ste/
22b4(phal chen): nged kyis ji ltar shin tu rnam par phye ba de bzhin du/sangs rgyas thams cad kyis kyang shin tu rnam par phye'o//'di nyidu yang /khyod kyi chos bstan pa des/byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing
22b5(phal chen): stong gi rdul shin tu phra ba snying kyis byang chub tu sems bskyedo//de dag kyang nged kyis lung bstan te/de dag ni ma 'ongs pa'i dus/bskal pa stong phrag brjod du med pa'i mthar/thams cad kyang dus
22b6(phal chen): gcig tu 'jig rten gyi khams ming tha dad pa dag tu de bzhin gshegs pa mtshan gcig pa sha stag 'byung bar 'gyur te/'di ltar sems rnam par dag pa zhes bya ba sha stag tu 'gyuro//nged kyis chos bstan pa 'di
22b7(phal chen): yang ji ltar ma 'ongs pa'i dus na/ma 'ongs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ma thos pa rnams kyis thos par bya ba dang /ji ltar 'jig rten gyi khams mi mjed 'dir/sems can rnams kyis shes shing /gling bzhi pa
22b8(phal chen): 'di'i ri rab kyi rtse mo la/ji ltar sems can rnams 'dul bar gyur pa de bzhin du/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong tshad med pa grangs med pa/bsam gyis mi khyab pa/mtshungs pa med pa/
23a1(phal chen): @#//kun tu mtha' yas pa/dpag tu med pa/sa mtshams med pa/brjod du med pa/chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa/nam mkha'i mthas gtugs pa dag tu yang sems can rnams 'dul bar 'gyur 
23a2(phal chen): bar bya ba'i phyir byin gyis brlab po/thams cad na yang chos bstan pa 'di nyid/byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros shes bya ba sha stag/sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /bcom ldan 'das kyi sngon gyi smon lam gyis/
23a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad yang dag par bstan pa dang /ye shes kyi snang ba shin tu gsal bar bya ba dang /don gyis gdul ba'i snang ba thob pa dang /chos nyid rab tu 'thob par bya ba dang /byang chub sems dpa'
23a4(phal chen): rnams shin tu dga' bar bya ba dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos kyi rgyu yang dag par bstan pa dag /sangs rgyas kyi chos mnyam pa nyid du bya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings gnyisu med pa la 'jug par bya ba'i phyir rab tu ston to//
23a5(phal chen): de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos gyi blo gros kyis phyogs bcu/bltas te/'khor thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/nam mkha'i khams la ni rnam par dpyod/sems can gyi khams ni yongs 
23a6(phal chen): su smin par byed/las dang rnam par smin pa'i khams ni 'gal bar mi byed/nam mkha'i khams ni yongasu dag par byed/kun nas nyon mongs pa las byung ba ni rab tu sel/mos pa yangs pa las byung ba ni shin tu 'phel 
23a7(phal chen): bar byed/sems can gyi dbang po tha dad pa la rab tu 'jug/dus gsum ni mnyam par byed/mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings la ni yang dag par rtog cing /rang gi dge ba'i rtsa ba'i stobs yongasu dag par bya ba la gzhol 
23a8(phal chen): ba'i phyir/de'i tshe sangs rgyas kyi mthus tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//gang zhig phyogs rnams bcu po ma lus rgyas byas shing /sems can zhing dang chos gsum dag kyang rtogs gyur pa/sangs rgyas mtsho mchog 
23b1(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin te//de dag 'jig rten phan phyir sems kyi dam pa skyed//nam mkha'i dbyings kyis klas pa'i chos dbyings ji bzhin dang //ji ltar sangs rgyas chos rnams 'gro kun rjes song dang //sangs rgyas thams
23b2(phal chen): cad kun tu khyab bsnyes ji bzhin gyi//'gro ba mi ldog don du phan par rab tu zhugs//'jig rten rnams la byams pas rtag par rab gnas shing //'tshe ba med pa 'gro la phan phyir su zhugs te//snang ba mi zad 'od byed
23b3(phal chen): gnasu gyur pa rnams//bsam gyis mi khyab stobs bcu mnga' bas byin gyis brlabs//de ni zhing rnams tha dad ma yin tshul yang rnyed//de ni lus kyi rnam pa kun la rab tu zhugs//bsod nams ye shes de yang
23b4(phal chen): sangs rgyas byang chub bzhin//de ni chags med gzhol ba ye shes rgyur skyeso//spyi'u tshugs rgyu ba'i 'gro ba sna tshogs ma lus par//gzhan yang zhing rnams 'byam klas chags med mthar thug pa//de dag
23b5(phal chen): phra sbom rgya che yangs pa thams cadu//byang chub sems mchog de ni gcig pu kun tu 'gro//brjodu med pa shin tu phra zhing mig nas pa'i//byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa'i mchog la rtag zhugs shing //
23b6(phal chen): mkhas pa gang gis sangs rgyas chos mthong mi ngoms pa//de dag chos rnams rgya mtsho'i rnam par chud pa yin//'jig rten lam rgyud kun la su zhig snying brtse zhing //'jig rten gsum po sbyang par rtag tu
23b7(phal chen): zhugs pa'i rnams//phyi mi'i mthar thug sangs rgyas gdung gi rgyun mi gcod//de ni bdud kyi khyim yang rab tu 'joms pa yin//dus gsum rgyal ba rnams dang rgyud gcig dngos gyur cing //shin tu phra ba'i tshul 
23b8(phal chen): gyis lam la rtag brtson te//su zhig sangs rgyas yul la dad sems pa//de ni chags med thub pa kun gyis dbang yang bskur//rdo rje lta bur sra zhing brtan la mi shigs pa//rkang gnyis gtso bos mdzad pa rtag tu
24a1(phal chen): @#//dran shes shing //'dren pa'i spyod yul snang rab shin tu rnyed pa'i rnams//gzhan gyi dring la mi rgyug byang chub sems kyang ste//'gro ba rnams kyi 'du shes rnam pa mang shes shing //de bzhin las
24a2(phal chen): dang 'bras bu sems kyi rnams kyang ste//gzhan yang dbang po'i mthu dang khams rnams du ma dag/kun kyang byang chub sems la spyod yul gcig yino//gang zhig chos kyi dbyings bzhin byang chub sems yangs shing //
24a3(phal chen): rtag tu chos la mi gnas namakha' ji bzhin pa//chags med sems ldan de dag sangs rgyas ye shes zhugs//de dag rtog med chos can yang dag mtha' la gnas//sems can bsam pa rnam 'byed sems can 'du
24a4(phal chen): shes med//chos kyi rnam rtag khrag khrig pha mthar rab tu phyin//de dag rtog la mos shing rnam par mi rtog ste//de dag zhing rnams bye ba khrag khrig rjesu zhugs//gang zhig sangs rgyas bye ba
24a5(phal chen): khrag khrig rjes sems shing //gang zhig chos dbyings tshul gyi mdzod du zhugs gyur la//gang zhig sems can dbang po'i rnam pa'i mthar phyin pa//de dag sems can gtso bzhin chos nyid shes
24a6(phal chen): pa yin//lhag pa'i bsam pa dge dang rtag par mngon ldan zhing //skyes bu'i seng ge'i bsnyen bkur phyir ldog mi 'gyur la//'jig rten gtso bo mkhas pa mthong bas mi ngoms pa//de dag sangs rgyas khrag khrig bye
24a7(phal chen): bas byin gyis bslabs//zab mo'i chos rnams rnam par 'byed kyang chags pa med//gnas pa med pas sems kyi dam pa rtag tu dag/de dag bskal pa mtha' yas skyes bu seng+ge'i sems//dus gsum chags pa
24a8(phal chen): med par rtogs pa'i yid dang ldan//mi 'phrogs thub par mi nus yid kyi dbang po dang //gsum pa rjesu song dang rtag tu ldan gyur cing //chos rnams 'byam klas shin tu bslabs pas rmongs med pa//de dag chos
24b1(phal chen): dbyings 'gengs kyang nam yang mi ngom so//tshig gi lam 'das zhi bzhin mkhas pa zhi ba shes//de bzhin nyid la nam yang rnam par mi 'byed pa//de dag sangs rgyas thams cad yul la rjes sems shing //dus
24b2(phal chen): gsum chags pa med pa rtogs pa'i yid ldan 'gyur//mkhas pa gang zhig sems bskyed 'byam klas chos kyi dbyings//'gengs shing phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas zhing du 'gro ba ni//de dag khrag khrig tshul 
24b3(phal chen): rnams brjodu med pa yis//snang ba rnyed par gnas pa'i rgyal ba'i sras yino//chos rab mu stegs snying rje'i tshul gyis rjes song zhing //byams pa'i pha rol phyin pa bar snang ji bzhin te//sems can khams 
24b4(phal chen): la nams kyang yongasu rtog med pa//de dag rnam rtog med la brtson zhing 'jig rten spyod//phyogs bcu dag tu bden pas 'gro ba yid 'bebs par//'dod pas sems can gtso bo de dag kun tu rgyu//
24b5(phal chen): bden pa'i tshig brtson rtag tu nges pa'i sems ldan pa//de dag phyogs bcu'i mi'i mchog gis byin gyis brlabs//'das par gyur pa'i dus kyang de dag rjesu dran//de bzhin ma 'ongs dus
24b6(phal chen): la 'jug pa rnam par bsgom//de dag phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten kun na mi gnas te//sems can phan dgrol phyir kun tu rnam par rgyu//ye shes 'od mchog dam pas chags pa med gyur pa//rgyu yi rnam pa 
24b7(phal chen): chos rab nem nur med brtan rnams//de dag the tsom thams cad rnam par zlog pa byed//chos kyi dbyings 'dir nem nur med par rab tu 'jug/gang zhig bdud rnams kun gyi gnas rnams 'jom byed dang //gang 
24b8(phal chen): yang sems can rab rib shin tu sel byed dang //gang rnams rtogs pa 'jig cing rnams rtog mi 'grogs pa//sems can gtso bo de dag yul la the tshom med//dus gsum ma lus the tsom rnam par 
25a1(phal chen): @#//zlog byas shig/bsam pa'i mchog gis de bzhin gshegs pa'i drung 'gro ste//dad pa'i stobs dang ldan zhing blo brtan rnyed pa'i rnams//de dag don rtogs khong du chud cing blo yang dag/
25a2(phal chen): mkhas pa 'gro ba'i don du phyi ma'i mthar spro ste//sems can don du rnam thar 'byam klas yongasu tshol//yun ring dus su skyo ba med cing zhum pa med//sems can dmyal ba sdug bsngal mang po zlog par byed//
25a3(phal chen): de dag bsod nams mi zad ye shes ldan//'gro ba'i dbang po sna tshogs bsam la rab tu 'jug/skye dgu'i las kyi rgyud kyang shin tu shes byas shing //bsam pa ji bzhin tshul 'dra'i chos rnams mtha' 
25a4(phal chen): yas brjod//'gro la byams pa'i sa rnams kun dang rjesu ldan//bdag med nyid rtogs 'on kyang btang snyoms byed pa min//de dag snying brtse gcig tu gsal bar chos kyang brjod//'on kyang dmigs 
25a5(phal chen): par mi gnas de dag 'jig rten rgyu//de dag 'od zer dra ba sna tshogs sa mdog bkye zhing //'gro ba'i mun pa bsal te kun tu snang byas nas//'od zer sgo'i pad+mo dag la rgyal sras rnams//
25a6(phal chen): kun tu bstan de rdul dang bral ba'i chos kyang ston//spu gcig rtse la zhing rnams rgyal bas brgyan pa dang //de bzhin rgyal ba'i sras kyis brgyan pa mngon par mthong //'khor rnams blo sems gzhan dang gzhan 
25a7(phal chen): can dpe med pa//'gro ba de dag sems shes rmongs med ston pa byed//sems 'jug gcig gis phyogs rnams bcu po thams cad kyi//brjod du med pa khrag khrig zhing rnams kun bgrod cing //song ste sangs 
25a8(phal chen): rgyas 'gro ba'i don du mchod byas nas//dri ba khrag khrig don dang ldan pa sna tshogs 'dri//bde gshegs rnams la pha'i 'du shes nyer bzhag cing //chos kyi gter mkhas rtag tu shes rab rnon po can//
25b1(phal chen): ye shes 'byung gnas mkhas pas sangs rgyas chags med phyir//'gro ba'i don 'dod byang chub mchog gi spyod pa sgrub//gang rnams chos dbyings rnam par phye zhing bsam byas kyang //bskal pa mang po 'das 
25b2(phal chen): byas zad par ma gyur te//chos nyid gnas med gang la'ang chud par ma gyur kyang //chags med ye shes can rnams skyo sems med par spyod//dus gsum rgyal ba'i rigasu rigs dang ldan gyur cing //
25b3(phal chen): bde gshegs kun gyi sku las yang dag byung gyur pas//chos kyi lus can de dag 'gro la gzugs lus ston//sgyu mkhan mkhas pa yid 'ong sgyu ma ston pa bzhin//mkhas pa de dag la lar spyod pa 
25b4(phal chen): mchog rnyed snang //la lar mkhas pa skye zhing mya ngan 'das par snang //byang chub dam pa'i mchog kyang rnam par 'tshang rgya snang //mya ngan 'da' ba ston pa'ang de bzhin la lar snang //rang rgyal rnams dang 
25b5(phal chen): nyan thos rgyal ba'i sa min pa'i//chos nyid de la ji ltar rab tu gnas pa ltos//lus dang sgra med bsam med 'du shes ldan pa min//'on kyang bya ba sna tshogs du ma nye bar ston//
25b6(phal chen): 'gro bar chos rnams rnam par 'byed cing skyer 'gyur te//'jig rton gtso bo mi bzhugs phyin rgyal sras rnams//chags med mthar phyin pa'i ye shes gnas bcas te//sangs rgyas byin brlabs rnam par 'phrul ba dag
25b7(phal chen): kyang ston//'jig rten 'di na de dag 'dra ba gang yang med//de dag pas kyang 'phags pa lta go ji zhig smros//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes yangs pa ma thob par//thams cad mkhyen pa'i thabasu rnam 'phrul mchog kyang 
25b8(phal chen): thob//gang rnams chos rab 'phags par rtag tu 'jug byed pa//de dag chos rab dam pa mchog gi theg pa yin//'jig rten dus dang dus min tshod kyang shes byas shing //de dag 'gro la phan phyir rnam par 'phrul pa ston/
26a1(phal chen): @#//dpa' bo gang rnams zhing kun bdag gi lus 'gengs shing //gang rnams 'jig rten 'od zer dra ba mchog gtong pa//mun pa bsal cing snang bas 'jig rten rgyas par byed//klu'i dbang 
26a2(phal chen): po bzhin du chos kyi char pa 'bebs//rmi lam lta bu log pa'i sgyu ma 'dra ba yi//'du byed rab tu shes nas las rnams sbyor 'jug cing //'gro ba thams cad snying rje chen pos khyab byas nas//rang bzhin dag 
26a3(phal chen): pa thar lam bya ba med pa ston//gang rnams rgyal ba'i stobs rnams ji bzhin tshad med dang //gang rnams mkha' ltar 'gro ba kun tu mtha' yas shing //gang rnams sems can khams kun yongs su dgrol brtson 
26a4(phal chen): pa||de dag bskal pa bye ba grangs med spyad pa spyod//las mchog bla na med pa shin tu sbyang pa yis//yon tan dam pa sna tshogs rnams kyang kun sems shing //byang chub sems dpa' de dag spyod pa 
26a5(phal chen): mchog mi mthong //thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes 'byung ba rnam par lta//sems 'byung gcig gis 'jig rten bsam gyis mi khyab pa//'byam klas kun tu rgyu zhing 'gro ba rab tu snang //
26a6(phal chen): rtog dang rnam rtog mi byed dag pa'i sems dang ldan//de dag gcig min lus la lus gcig ston pa byed//de dag 'jig rten kun la rtog par mi byed de//mgon po kun gyi chos nyid yongs su mi rtog go/de dag chos 
26a7(phal chen): kun rnams 'byed 'dod chags bral gyur kyang //'gro ba thar par bya ba yongs su mi gtong ngo//'jig rten kun gyi 'du shes rnam pa gang yod pa//bzang ngan 'bring dang de bzhin chung dang sna tshogs kyis//'du shes thibs 
26a8(phal chen): po dag dang rab rgal rnam phye ste//de dag byin brlabs rnam par 'phrul ba shin tu ston//ji ltar sgyu ma rnam pa sna tshogs tshad med pa//de 'dra'i rnam par 'phrul bas chos dbyings 'di dag 'gengs//nam mkha'i 
26b1(phal chen): dbyings kyi mthas klas lus kyi bye brag gis//'gro ba rnams kyi mos pa ji bzhin ston pa byed//de dag gang la'ang ma chags rtogs dang rnam rtog med//dog pa med cing nyon mongs de bzhin rlom sems med//
26b2(phal chen): de dag sangs rgyas chags pa'i blo med thar la min//'gro ba kun la'ang bde ba'i 'du shes rab tu skyes//'jig rten kun na shes la mkhas rab mi 'jigs pa//'jig rten 'du shes gnas la 'jug par mi byede//
26b3(phal chen): de dag chos kun 'di la 'du shes gang zhes rtog/de ltar gzhigs na dus gsum gang yang dmigs pa med//sngon gyi mthar thug 'das pa'i dus la rab zhugs te//phyi ma'i mthar thug ma 'ongs pa la rab zhugs
26b4(phal chen): nas||zhing rnams kun na ci yod kun kyang de bzhin du//shin tu shes kyang rtag par gang yang mi dmigs so//mya ngan 'das pa'i dbyings la'ang brtson par de bzhin gnas//nyon mongs med 
26b5(phal chen): pa'i tshul gyi rjes song mi gnas te//de dag yang dag ma tha' 'dra de la 'dra ba med//byang chub spyod pa las kyang dpa' bo mi ldog go/gang rnams byang chub brtan pa'i ri rab bzhin gnas shing //
26b6(phal chen): sangs rgyas rgyal sras mkhas pas chos dbyings thams cad dang //'gro ba kun tu shin tu gang byas pa//'di ni dpa' bo rnams kyi spyod pa'i mchog yino//blo mchog dam pa gang zhig thams cad mkhyen pa yi//
26b7(phal chen): rnam grol mdzes pa'i tshul mchog yangs pa 'dod pa ni//des ni gang las zag zad srid gsum bde 'byung ba'i//byang chub sems la rim pa med par 'jug par gyis//gang gi bsam pa rdul bral byang chub 
26b8(phal chen): sems gnas pa//de dag yon tan legs pa zad par brjod mi nus//'on kyang 'gro ba'i don du bde gshegs legs brjod phyir//yon tan ldan pa de'i spyod pa mchog kyang nyon//gang gis zhing rnams bye ba khrag 
27a1(phal chen): @#//khrig rdul byas te//phyogs rnams re re kun tu bkang ste bgrang byas shing //de'i rjesu dpag pas rnam 'dren zad byed na//'du shes rnams ni mthar thugs zad par mi 'gyuro//gang zhig sems 
27a2(phal chen): can grangs mkhas sems can 'du shes med spyod pa tsam du gzhol zhing sgra la ma chags pa//gang zhig 'jig rten mkhams 'byed chags pa med pa'i sems//de dag ldang dang grangs mkhas grangs dmigs med pa yin//
27a3(phal chen): yid kyis namakha'i dbyangs ni mtha' yas rgya che dang //dus rnams 'jig rten kun tu rtogs pa'i mkhas pa gang //spar mos thal gtor bzhin du the tsom rnam gtor ba//de dag sangs rgyas chos rtogs yid can chags 
27a4(phal chen): pa med//gang dag phyogs rnams mtha' yas zhing la rab 'jug cing //sems bskyed gcig gis blo nyams med par 'dod byed pa//de dag tha dad grangs ltogs pas der zhugs te//skye ba med pa'i chos la yang 
27a5(phal chen): dag mtha' ltar mkhas//gang zhig sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig bas ma yas pa'i//'khor du nye bar phyin te zham ring 'khor gnas shing //gtso bo dag la 'dri ba'i thog mar gang 'gro ba//
27a6(phal chen): spyod yul mchog can de'i smon lam mchog 'di yin//gang zhig phyogs rnams kun gyi mgon po kun dran zhing //chos nyid de la nam yang gnas par ma gyur pa//khams gsum dge ba gang yod kun gyi rgyu yin te//
27a7(phal chen): de dag zhing rnams thams cad yongasu dag par sbyong //gang rnams skye ba'i rgyu du srid gsum sna tshogs rnams//'jig rten kun na yod la ma chags mig gyur pa//de dag bag chags mkhas shing de bzhin dbang po 
27a8(phal chen): mkhas//mos pa bye ba khrag khrig bsam yas de bzhin mkhas//'gro ba'i bsam pa ji bzhin gang gis shes gyur cing //shes rab chen po de dag de bzhin chos ston pa//de dag kun nas nyon mongs rnam dag 
27b1(phal chen): tshul la mkhas//sems can mang po rnam par sbyong ba'i lam ldan ston//gang zhig tinge 'dzin bye ba khrag khrig brjod byas rnams//sems kyi rgyu ba gcig gis rjesu zhugs gyur pa//de dag mtshan ma sna tshogs gnas
27b2(phal chen): par rab tu shes//ldang ba'i rgyu la mkhas shing blo gro spyo yul shes//chags med byang chub de la mngon du gang zhugs shing //'gro la phan pa'i don du blo yangasu zhugs pa//ye shes 'byam klas 'di dang de dag ldan
27b3(phal chen): pa yin//de phyir de dag skyes bu mchog gi gsung yang sgrog/gang zhig bskal pa ring dang bskal pa thung mkhas shing //nyin zhag mkhas shing de bzhin zla ba zla phyed mkhas//gang yang 'jig rten rtag tu skye ba mkhas
27b4(phal chen): gyur pa//zhing gi rnam pa mkhas shing rnam phye'i chos med pa'o//gang rnams phyogs la zhugs te phyogs kyi rjes 'brang zhing //phyogs kun tshad ni rjesu shes par ma gyur pa//de dag dbu mas phyogs kyi rjesu
27b5(phal chen): 'gro byed cing //zhing rnams sbyong yang yongasu dag rtogs chags pa med//stobs la 'jug pas rgyal ba rnams kyi stobs shes pas//'gro ba rnams kyi gnas min bsam byas na//'das dang ma 'ongs
27b6(phal chen): da ltar byung ba'i las kyi sa//yang dag rtogs byas las kyi rnam par smin la gnas//'jig rten kun mkhas sna tshogs tha dad ji bzhin du//srid gsum rnam par rgyu ba'i tshul yang ji bzhin dang //dbang po rnon po 'bring
27b7(phal chen): dang dbang po tha mar yang //'gro ba ma lus kun gyi dbang po rnam par 'byed//mos pa rnam pa sna tshogs dge dang mi dge dang //bzang ngan de bzhin 'bring la rnam par sems byed cing //'gro ba'i kun tu 'gro ba rnam par
27b8(phal chen): 'byed//srid gsum rgyu'i rgyud kyang de dag rab tu shes//bsam gtan rnam thar nyon mongs ji bzhin mtshan ma mthong //nyon mongs mtshams sbyor rnam byang cis 'gyur dang //sngan chad gnas kyi bde dang sdug bsngal
28a1(phal chen): @#//rgyud rnams kyang //brtan pa rnams ni sangs rgyas stobs rnams sbyong ba byed//ji ltar 'gro ba'i lam rgyud nyon mongs las 'jug dang //'gro rgyud thams cad 'jig cing zhing 'gyur thams cad 
28a2(phal chen): dang //zag pa sna tshogs rnams kyang ji ltar skyer 'gyur ba//bag chags mtshams sbyor ji bzhin de bzhin rab tu 'jug/ji ltar 'jig rten nyon mongs zad cing dag 'gyur ba//de la skyes bu khyu mchog spyod pa'i blo dang ldan//
28a3(phal chen): stobs 'chang dag ni stobs bcu rnams kyi 'od snang ste//rgyal ba'i stobs kyang thob min the tsom yod pa min//spu rtse gcig la gang rnams rnam par sems byed cing //la la nyon mongs la la dag dang yangs pa yi//sna 
28a4(phal chen): tshogs zhing rnams tshad las 'das pa kun mthong nas//las dang bya ba sna tshogs rgyud tu rab tu shes//gang rnams rdul gcig rtse la rnam sems brjod med pa'i//zhing dang bde gshegs sras rnams 
28a5(phal chen): sems can gtso mthong yang //zhing rnams 'khrugs shing 'dres pa'i nyes pa gang yang med//gcig la 'jug pas rnal 'byor ldan pa kun la 'jug/spu gcig rtse la phyogs bcu thams 
28a6(phal chen): cad rnams phye ste//nam mkha'i dbyings thugs 'jig rten khams rnams kun zhugs shing //phyogs 'ga' tsam yang rgyal bas stong phra mthong ba med//de bzhin zhing dang 'jig rten 'byam klas de dag sbyong //
28a7(phal chen): bam po nyi shu bzhi pa//de ltar brtan pas rgyal bas stong min zhing shes nas//'jig rten kun kyang spu rtse gcig la yod par 'jug/dus gsum rgyud rnams mtshan nyid tha dad khams kun dang //
28a8(phal chen): nyin zhag dus dang zla phyed rgyud kyi rnam thar 'jug/da ltar rgyal sras 'jigs med byang chub sems dpa'i rnams//skyes bu khyu mchog ye shes rgyal srid thob zhugs pa//de dag 'di 'dra shes nas rjes su 
28b1(phal chen): sems byed cing //bde gshegs rnams la dga' ba mtha' yas yangs pa 'thob//gang rnams bye ba khrag khrig sna tshogs lus bsgyur cing //phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba thams cad mngon par mchod pa'i phyir//rnam 
28b2(phal chen): 'phrul dam pa bzang po kun tu ston byed pa//de dag mgon po rnams kyi spyod yul rab la zhugs//gang rnams sangs rgyas bye ba khrag khrig drung bslabs zhing //gang rnams chos kyi mdzod rtogs yid la ngoms med de//
28b3(phal chen): gang dag mgon po khyu mchog rnams la dga' sems pa//de dag chos kyi sprin yin spyod pa mang la spyod//ye shes 'phel bar 'gyur ba'i chos kyi gnas mkhas pa//de dag sangs rgyas 'byung gnas ting 'dzin 
28b4(phal chen): thob pa yin//ri rgyal lta bu de dag dad pa'i 'byung gnas rten//ye shes 'byung gnas 'gro ba'i yon tan 'byung gnas so//gnas pa med pa dam pa shin tu yangs pa dang //thams cad mkhyen la mngon gyur mya 
28b5(phal chen): ngan med pa dang //sems can skyobs cing nyon mongs rnyog pa med zhugs pa'i//byams pa'i bsam pas 'gro ba kun kyang 'gengs//de dag snying rje'i blo yang shin tu yangs pa yin//bdag la 
28b6(phal chen): cir sems de bzhin sangs rgyas sems can la//gzhan du nam mkha' lta bur mtshan nyid med par 'jug/nyams par gyur pa'i sems can rnams mthong phyir zhing rtsom//byang chub sems la mkhas pa dag gi tshad dag 
28b7(phal chen): ni//bskal pa bye ba'i mthas kyang brjod par mi nus te//ci phyir zhe na de las sangs rgyas thams cad byung //dbang ba des rim gror byas dang rang rgyal kun kyang byung //phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams grangs med bcur ni 
28b8(phal chen): 'grangs gyur pa'i//sems can thams cad bskal pa gcig tu bder bkod cing //bslab pa bcu dang dge bcu bsam gtan 'thob pa dang //tshangs pa dag dang rnam grol zhi ba'i gnas bkod cing //de dag bskal pa bye 
29a1(phal chen): @#//bar bde bar rab bkod de//dgra bcom nyon mongs zhi la rab tu dgod byas kyang //byang chub sems dpa' dge ba'i tshogs kyi char mi rung //byang chub sems dpa'i zhugs ma thag tu tshogs chen sog
29a2(phal chen): nyon mongs med cing shin tu phra lam rtogs pa yi//rang rgyal sems can bye ba khrag khrig bkod gyur kyang //bsod nams bsags pa des ni byang chub sems rab la//grangs dang cha dang bgrang ba'i rnam par nyer mi 'gro /zhing gi
29a3(phal chen): rdul ni tshad kyis shes par rung yang srid//skad cig sems kyis zhing mang yongasu 'das byas shing //de ltar bskal pa brjod yas zad par 'gro byas na//zhing mtha' thug par rung srid byang chub sems mchog min//mtha'
29a4(phal chen): rnams kun tu bskal pa mang po'i mtha' med de//sngon gyi mthar gyur bskal pa de bzhin ma 'ongs dang //da ltar byung ba'i bskal pa zad dang thug med pa'i//bskal pa de dag zad srid byang chub sems ma yin//
29a5(phal chen): ci phyir zhe na 'di ltar phyogs rnams kun bkang zhing //rtog dang rnam rtog yongasu shes par bya ba'i phyir//skad cig gcig gis sa du gsum rtogs par bya don du//'gro ba'i don du 'dod cing sems
29a6(phal chen): mchog rin chen skye//phyogs bcu'i zhing rnams stong mang kun tu mtha' yas pa'i//'gro ba rnams kyi mos pa bsam pa ci 'dra dang //sbyor ba gang dang sems dang spyod pa de bzhin te//mthar thug rung srid mkha'
29a7(phal chen): 'dra'i byang chub sems mtha' min//de ni 'di ltar phyogs bcu byams dang snying rje dang //bsam pa chen pos 'gro ba thams cad rgyas byas shing //thar pa'i don du sangs rgyas yon tan kun sbyar phyir//rin chen sems
29a8(phal chen): kyi stobs ni tshad med rab tu skyes//mos dang bsam pa yid bzhin yang dag sbyor ba dang //'gro ba'i dbang po sbyor ba sems dang spyod pa rnams//skad cig gcig gis ji bzhin rtogs par bya ba'i phyir//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye
29b1(phal chen): shes lta bu'i sems skyeso//'gro ba'i las dang nyon mongs sna tshogs 'byam klas pa//gang gis khams gsum 'di dag tu ni skyer 'gyur pa//de dag mtha' ni phyogs bcur rnyed par rung srid kyi//byang chub sems kyi mchog
29b2(phal chen): la kun tu mtha' medo//ci phyir zhe na 'di ltar nyon mongs las bral ba'i//sems ni de bzhin gshegs pa mchod pa'i don du skye//nyon mongs grol byed de bzhin las kyi rgyud kyang gcod//dus gsum mun pa sel cing mi
29b3(phal chen): zad snying rjes byung //phyogs bcu'i sangs rgyas mtha' yas ma lus thams cad dang //sems can mang po thams cad sems gcig bskyed pa yis//spos dang me tog phreng ba byug pa dam pa dang //gdugs dang rgyal
29b4(phal chen): mtshan bza' btung na bzas mchod par byed//gzhan yang gdan gyi rnam pa 'chags dam pa dang //pho brang mang po sna tshogs tsan dan mchog bsangs pa//nor bu rin chen rnam par snang bas brgyan pa dang //yid
29b5(phal chen): bzhin nor bu yid 'ong brgyan pas mchod byas te//de ltar bde gshegs sems can mngon par mchod byed pas//bskal pa khrag khrig brjod du med pa zad byas kyang //byang chub sems dpa'i bde
29b6(phal chen): bsags mthar phyin bde ba nyid//gang bsgrubs byang chub sems la mnyam par nyer mi 'gro//ji snyed dper brjod byas pa 'di dag thams cad kyang //yang dag skyes pa'i byang chub sems kyi char mi rung //de yang
29b7(phal chen): 'di ltar dus gsum bzhugs pa'i skyes bu'i mchog/thams cad byang chub sems mchog de las byung ba yin//byang chub sems ni chags pa med pa'i mthar phyin te//thams cad tshad la gnas kyang de la tshad yod
29b8(phal chen): min//thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes gzhol zhing tshad med stobs//sems can rang bzhin de bzhin byang chub mthar yang phyin//byang chub sems 'di namakha' mtha' dang mnyam pa ste//chos rnams kun la khyad pa
30a1(phal chen): @#//chos dbyings ji bzhino//tshad med de bzhin nyid kun de'i spyod yul gyur//des ni kun tu khyab pa sems can gtso bo bzhin//de ni 'byam klas chos rnams kun la rjesu zhugs//des ni zhing rnams ma 
30a2(phal chen): lus 'byam klas kun tu khyab//des ni shes pa 'byam klas thams cad rtogs par chud//des ni sangs rgyas 'byam klas kun dang 'gal med thob//rtag tu bsod nams bla na med pa dag dang ldan//de bzhin rtag tu tshul 
30a3(phal chen): khrims rnam dag mi gnas ldan//rtag tu kun gtong rtag par rab tu dge ba ste//ldog pa med pa'i brtson 'grus mchog dang rab tu ldan//bsam gtan mdzod la rtag par bsam gtan 'jug pa byed//shes rab shin tu 
30a4(phal chen): yangs pa dang yang rtag tu ldan//rgyal sras rnams kyi sa rab dam pa gang yod pa//de dag don du ye shes kun tu bzang po byung //'gro ba kun na skyes bu'i seng+ge gang bzhugs pa//de dag gis ni byang chub sems rab 
30a5(phal chen): byin gyis brlabs//tinge 'dzin dang gzungs kyi dam pas rab tu brgyan//de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam 'phrul rdzu 'phrul rnam pas brgyan//'jig rten kun dang 'gro ba rnams kyi ji tsam tshad//
30a6(phal chen): namakha'i tshad kyang phyogs bcu dag tu ji tsam pa//de dag pas kyang byang chub sems 'di tshad med de//de las skyes bu'i seng ge kun kyang skyes shing byung //byang chub sems 'di stobs bcu rnams kyi rtsa ba ste//brtan
30a7(phal chen): pa bzhi'i gnas yin so sor shes pa'i rtsa//rgyal ba'i chos rnams bcwa brgyad yang dag 'byin pa ste//skyes bu gtso bos mkhyen par bya ba'i chos kun 'byin//gzugs kyi sku ni mtshan rab rnams kyis bsags pa dang //
30a8(phal chen): chos sku mnyam nyid rtog pa mi mnga' rnyes pa dang //'gro bas mchod pa'i ye shes chags med gang byung ba//kun gyi rtsa ba 'byung gnas byang chub sems 'di yin//rang rgyal theg pa 'byung zhing nyan thos theg pa'ang 'byung //
30b1(phal chen): bsam gtan ting 'dzin bde ba gzugs kyi khams rnams dang //gzugs med khams rnams tinge 'dzin gyi bde ba dag/kun gyi rtsa ba 'byung gnas byang chub sems mchog 'di yino//mi dang de bzhin lha'i dbang po'i bde ba dang //
30b2(phal chen): lam rgyud sna tshogs kun kyang 'di la shin tu sbyangs//brtson 'grus stobs dang dbang po'i bde ba gang yod pa//kun gyi rtsa ba dang po byang chub sems 'di yin//ci phyir zhe na 'di ltar byang chub sems bskyed cing //pha rol phyin
30b3(phal chen): drug brtson par spyod pa rab tu spyod//sems can rnams la'ang spyod pa yang dag rab tu ston//des ni khams gsum 'gro ba'i bde ba kun kyang 'byung //sangs rgyas chags med thugs chung don dam mkhyen pa'i rnams//chos
30b4(phal chen): kyi dam pa mchog rnams yang dag kun ston cing //rgya chen nyon mongs las las sems can dgrol mdzad//mya ngan 'das pa dam pa mchog la dgod mdzad pa//ye shes 'od zer 'od yangs nyi ma lta bu ste//
30b5(phal chen): 'gro bar spyod pa dkar pos yongs rgyas zla ba bzhin//yon tan shin tu bsags pa tshad med chu lta bu//dri med chags pa mi mnga' tshad med mkha' dbyings 'dra//gang zhig yon tan mtha' yas byang
30b6(phal chen): chub sems yangs pa//shin tu che zhing bde skyed tshad med smon byed cing //phyi ma'i mthar thug spyod par smon pas su brtson pa//de dag sems can gdul ba'i don phyir rtag tu brtson//'jig rten 'jam klas thams cad
30b7(phal chen): rnam par sbyang ba'i phyir//smon lam bye ba khrag khrig bsam gyis mi khyab dang //stong pa mtshan ma med pa smon med mi gnas pa'i//smon lam bye ba brgyas ni sems de dag par 'gyur//chos kyi rang bzhin namakha'
30b8(phal chen): lta bu sus rtogs shing //zhi dang rab zhi nyer zhir thams cad mnyam gzhog pa//de dag chos grangs khrag khrig bsam gyis mi khya ba'i//grangasu 'gro la ston kyang de ni gnas pa med//phyogs bcu kun gyi 'jig
31a1(phal chen): rten gtso bo ji snyed pa//de dag kun gyis byang chub sems mchog 'di bsngags ste//yon tan brgya phrag bsam gyis mi khyab pas kyang brgyan//sems can gtso bzhin yongs dag pha rol phyin
31a2(phal chen): pa yin//bskal pa sems can snye dug la te legs brjod kyang //byang chub sems rab de la mthar thugs gang yang med//rgyal ba 'byung ba'i rigs chen der ni gang skyes pa//de'i dpe ni khams gsum klongs kyang gang yang
31a3(phal chen): med//dus gsum rgyal ba kun gyis gang zhig rnam bsags pa'i//byang chub sems rab nge la smon lam kun kyi gyis//byang chub sems rab dam pa de'i bsod nams gter//des ni rgyal sras chags pa med pa rab tu
31a4(phal chen): 'thob//'gro ba'i sems dang spyod pa shes shing mthong rung srid//zhing rnams rdul du pags te bgrangs par nus kyang srid//bar snang skra yis mnyam par gzhal bar nus srid kyi//byang chub sems
31a5(phal chen): mchog yon tan mthar phyin mi nuso//'di las dus gsum sangs rgyas thams cad rab tu byung //'di las 'jig rten 'gro ba kun gyi bde ba 'byung //'di las rgyal bas bsngags pa'i legs pa thaMd
31a6(phal chen): 'byung //'dis ni sems can gtso bo the tsom med par 'gyur//'dis ni 'jig rten kun gyi bsgrib pa zad par 'gyur//'dis ni zhing rnams yongasu dag pa'i mchog tu 'gyur//'di las 'jig rten dam pa shes dang
31a7(phal chen): ldan pa 'byung //gang zhig phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba blta bar 'dod pa dang //gang zhig byin dang bsod nams mi zad mdzod 'dod dang //gang zhig 'gro ba kun gyi sdug bsngal zlog 'dod pa//des ni yon tan mtha' yas
31a8(phal chen): byang chub sems bskyed bya//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo////sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las/byang chub sems dpa' sems dang po bskyed pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po'i dpe yang dag
31b1(phal chen): par bsags pa tshigasu bcad pa'i le'u zhes bya ste/nyi shu gnyis pa'o////de nas byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus blo gros kyis/byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros la 'di skad ces 
31b2(phal chen): smraso//kye rgyal ba'i sras/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi sems bskyed pa rab tu thob pa/sems dang po bskyed pa la gnas pa/de ltar tshad med pa'i bsod nams kyi tshogs dang ldan pa/thams cad
31b3(phal chen): mkhyen pa'i ye shes rgya chen po la yang dag par 'dzegs pa//byang chub sems dpa'i skyon med par shin tu chung pa//'jig rten kun tu khrid par byed pa rnam par bzlog pa//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi 'jig rten
31b4(phal chen): las 'das pa'i chos nyid kyi rjesu song ba/'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis byin gyis brlabs pa/byang chub sems dpa'i rnam par dgod pa la shin tu
31b5(phal chen): nges pa rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu rab tu gzhol ba de dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa bstan pa la ji ltar brtson par byas pa 'i rnam pa gang gis/de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad mnyes par 'gyur
31b6(phal chen): ba dang /byang chub sems dpa'i rnal par dgod pa las 'das par 'gyur ba dang /spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor chen po yongasu dag par 'gyur ba dang /smon lam chen po yongasu rdzogs par 'gyur ba dang /byang chub sem+sa
31b7(phal chen): dpa'i gter chen po rab tu thob par 'gyur ba dang /sems can rnams la snod ci bzhin du sbyin pa byed par 'gyur ba dang /pha rol tu phyin pa'i phyogs yongasu mi gtong bar 'gyur ba dang /ji ltar dam bcas pa'i
31b8(phal chen): sems can rnams sgrol bar 'gyur ba dang /sangs rgyas dang /chos dang /dge 'dun gyi gdung mi 'chad par 'gyur ba dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba dmigs pa thams cad du sbyor ba don med par mi 'gyur ba dang /kye rgyal
32a1(phal chen): @#//ba'i sras/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis de la brtson par byas nas/chos kyi yul 'di dag yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur bar ji ltar brtsams par bya ba de legs par bshad pa mdzod cig/
32a2(phal chen): 'khor 'di dag thams cad ni nyan par 'dod do//sems can chen po de dag gi brtson pa'i rnam pa ji lta bus ma rig pa'i mun nag thams cad bsal te/bdud kyi dkrugs ma'i 'khor thams cad las shin tu rgyal 
32a3(phal chen): bar byas nas/phas kyi rgol ba thams cad zil gyis mnan te/sems kyi dri ma kun nas nyon mongs pa'i phyogs thams cad kun nas bsal nas/dge ba'i rtsa ba phun sum tshogs pa thams cad shin tu bsgrubs
32a4(phal chen): ste|mi khoms pa dang /ngan song dang /ngan 'gro'i lam thams cad du rnam par bzlog nas/ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor chen po rnam par byang bar byas te dang /byang chub sems dpa'i sa dang /pha rol tu phyin pa 
32a5(phal chen): dang /gzungs dang /tinge 'dzin dang /mngon par shes pa dang /rig pa dang /mi 'jigs pa yongasu dag pa rnams mngon par bsgrubs nas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing thams cad rnam par dag pa dang /
32a6(phal chen): mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po dang /sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rgyan mngon par sgrub pa yongasu rgyas par byas te/de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyi stobs dang /mi 'jigs pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres 
32a7(phal chen): pa mnyam pa nyid kyi rjesu song ste/thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi yul rtog+sa par 'gyur ba dang /de ltar mngon par byang chub nas kyang /bsam pa'i tshul bzhin du/sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongasu grub par 
32a8(phal chen): gyur pa dag tu ji ltar yongasu smin par bya ba'i sems can dag la dus dang dbang po'i rgyud ji lta ba bzhin du/sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa mtha' yas pa 'byin par 'gyur ba de legs par shod cig/'khor 'di 
32b1(phal chen): ngag thams cad ni nyan par 'dod do//ji ltar yang byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis/byang chub sems dpa'i yon tan 'di dang gzhan dag dang /byang chub sems dpa'i chos dang /byang chub sems pa'i spyod pa dang /
32b2(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa'i lam gyi yul rnams yongasu rdzogs par byas te/myur du de bzhin gshegs pa'i mnyam pa nyid 'thob pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas 
32b3(phal chen): rnams kyis bskal pa brgya stong grangs med par yang dag par bsgrubs pa'i chos kyi mdzod 'di yang /yang dag par srung zhing byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa la gnas shing ston pa dang /'chad pa'i tshe/bdud dam/
32b4(phal chen): bdud kyi rigasu gtogs pa dang /phas kyi rgol ba thams cad kyis zil gyis mi non pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams thams cad yang skyo ba med par dam pa'i chos yongasu 'dzin par byed pa dang /
32b5(phal chen): de yang dag par ston pa'i tshe/lha'i dbang po rnams kyis rim gror bya ba dang /gzhung de bzhin du klu'i dbang po dang /gno/nga sbyin gyi dbang po dang /dri za'i dbang po dang /lha ma yin gyi dbang po dang /
32b6(phal chen): namakha' lding gi dbang po dang /mi 'am ci'i dbang po dang /lto 'phye chen po'i dbang po dang /mi'i dbang po dang /tshangs pa'i dbang pos rim gror bya ba rab tu 'thob pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa chos kyi dbang 
32b7(phal chen): pos rim gror bya ba rab du 'thob pa dang /de ltar rim gror byas nas kyang 'jig rten thams cad kyis phyag bya ba/'jig rten thams cad kyis mngon par mchod pa/'jig rten thams cad kyi dbang bskur ba/
32b8(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad kyis shin tu dgongs pa/byang chub sems dpa'i tshogs kyis shin tu bsrung ba/rang gi dge ba'i rtsa ba dang /smon lam rab tu thob pa/chos dkar po chen po yang dag par 
33a1(phal chen): spel ba'i rnams/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos kyi mdzod rnam par 'byed par 'gyur ba dang /dam pa'i chos yongs su 'dzin pa rgyan chen po rab tu 
33a2(phal chen): 'byed pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i rim gyis spyod pa yongs su bsgrub pa shin tu ston par 'gyur ba de lta bu'i gnas dag yang dag par bstan pa mdzod cig/de nas byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus blo gros kyis/don 'di nyid 
33a3(phal chen): brnan te brjod pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros la tshigs su bcad smras pa/spyod la 'jug pa mtha' yas yangs pa dag/rang byung ye shes sgrub pa dge ba'i rnams//rdzogs pa'i byang chub sems dpa' yon 
33a4(phal chen): tan can//grags pa chen po rnams la legs par shong //byang chub sems dpa' gang rnams sems thob cing //bsod nams ldan pa ye shes thob pa'i rnams//skyon med zhugs pa 'jig rten rnam bzlog 
33a5(phal chen): cing //yang dag sangs rgyas chos mnyam nyid thob rnams//de dag gis ni bde gshegs bstan pa la//brtson 'grus brtan pas ji ltar brtson par bya//gang gis phyogs bcu'i rgyal ba mnyes bya 
33a6(phal chen): zhing //yang dag sangs rgyassa'ang gnan bya dang //spyod pa dag dang smon lam yongs dgang dang //ye shes gter chen rab tu 'thob 'gyur zhing //thob nas de dag 'gro kun mi gnas pas//yongs su blangs pa rtag tu gtong byed 
33a7(phal chen): dang //gang yang pha rol phyin pa mchog gi lam//de dag mi gtong rab tu spyod pa dang //sems can ji ltar sgrol byed dam bcas shing //yang dag sangs rgyasabasam pa mnyam 'dzin dang //mig gi dmigs pa brtan pa thams 
33a8(phal chen): cad kyi//'byung ba yon tan can la stobs 'gyur zhing //skyes bu khyu mchog de dag brtson 'gyur pa'i//rnam dag lam de khyu mchog khyod kyis ston//ji ltar de dag mun pa kun bsal cing //'khrug pa'i 'khor rnams thams cad 
33b1(phal chen): pham byas nas//dri ma bral ba'i dge ldan mkhas pa rnams//skyes bu khyu mchog ye shes ldan 'gyur dang //mi khom ngan song lam rnams yongs spangs shing //ye shes chen po'i dkyil 'khor mchog sbyong dang //yon tan
33b2(phal chen): bzang po thams cad ldan gyur cing //rgyal ba'i ye shes stobs kyang thob 'gyur dang //skyes bu khyu mchog ye shas zhugs nas kyang //sems can bsam pa ji bzhin ston byed cing //mtha' yas yangs pa'i zhing rnams
33b3(phal chen): bsam yasu//ston pa chen po'i las kyang byed pa dang //ci nas de dag spyod pa mchog thob nas//mi'i khyu mchog mdzod rnams ston byed de//zil gyis ma non gzhan gyis mi 'pham par//rgyal ba'i chos nyid rtag tu 'dzin
33b4(phal chen): byed ston//seng ge bzhin du ji ltar 'jig pa med//ji ltar zla bzhin dag pa'i mdog tu spyod//chu la pad+mo bzhin du chags med pa//de dag rgyal ba'i yon tan ji ltar sbyong //de skad ces smras pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi
33b5(phal chen): blo gros kyis//byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus blo gros la 'di skad ces smraso//kye rgyal ba'i sras/khyod ni skye bo mang po la phan pa dang //skye bo mang po la bde ba dang /'jig rten la snying brtse ba
33b6(phal chen): dang /lha dang mi rnams kyi don dang /phan pa dang /bde ba'i phyir zhugs pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras/khyod de ltar byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa shin tu rtogs shing rnam par dag par brtsams te 'dri zhing /de ltar rgyal
33b7(phal chen): ba'i sras khyod de bzhin gshegs pa dri ba chos mang po la rab tu gnas pa/brtson 'grus chen pos shin tu bsags pa brtson 'grus ldog pa med pas shin tu byung ba 'dri bar byed pa ni/kye rgyal ba'i sras legaso legs
33b8(phal chen): so//kye rgyal ba'i sras/de'i phyir shin tu legs par nyon la yid la zung shig dang /sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs tsam bstan par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras 'di la/
34a1(phal chen): @#//byang chub sems dpa' thams cad mkhyen pa nyidu sems bskyed pa theb pa rnams kyis/thog mar sems bskyed pa de mi nyams par yongasu bsrungs pa dang /rim gror bya ba'i phyir bag yod 
34a2(phal chen): par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras de la rnam pa bcus ni/byang chub sems dpa' bag yod pa la gnas pa zhes byas te/bcu gang zhe na/tshul khrims kyi phung po shin tu bsrungs pa dang /byang chub kyi sems ma nyam+sa 
34a3(phal chen): shing shin tu sbyangs pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i bsam pa g.yo dang sgyu med pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba sbyor ba phyir mi ldog pa dang /rang gi sems bskyed pa shin tu rtogs pa dang /phyogs khyim pa dang /sau'i 
34a4(phal chen): skye bo dang ma 'dres pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba'i las 'jig rten gyi 'du shes dang 'dod pa thams cad dang bral ba dang /byang chub sems pa'i lam nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa spangs pa rab tu 
34a5(phal chen): thob pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa brtsam pa/nan tan rgyu na chad pa med pa dang /bdag gi rgyud kyi stobs kyi shugs shin tu brtags pa dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras rnam pa bcu po 'di dag dang 
34a6(phal chen): ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni bag yod pa la gnas pa zhes bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras de la byang chub sems dpa' bag yod pa la gnas pa rnam pa bcus ni/yongasu dag par 'gyur te/bcu gang zhe na/
34a7(phal chen): 'di lta ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras 'di la byang chub sems dpa' ji ltar smras pa de ltar byed pa yin/shes bzhin gyi dran pa can yin/khengs pa med cing g.yeng pa med de/bsam gtan zab mo la rab tu 
34a8(phal chen): gnas pa yin/sangs rgyas kyi chos zab mo btsal ba la nan tan du byed cing rgyun mi gcod pa yin/lhag mthong mngon par bsgrub pa la mkhas shing ji lta thos pa'i chos la tshul bzhin du rtog par 
34b1(phal chen): zhugs pa yin/bsam gtan gyi rjesu song ba'i sems kyis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul pa zab mo'i rjesu song ba yin/sa dang mnyam zhing mthon dman dang bral ba yin/sbyor ba bzang ngan 
34b2(phal chen): dang bar ma la sems mnyams pa med pa yin/sems can kun gyi nang na chung ngun thams cad mkhyen pa nyidu sems gcig bskyed pa la yang /ston pa'i bsam pa thob pa yin/mkhan po dang /slob dpon
34b3(phal chen): dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen dang /byang chub sems dpa' dang /chos smra ba rnams la rim 'gro dang /bkur sti dang /ngang tshul can du gnas pa yin te/kye rgyal ba'i sras rnam pa bcu po de dag gis/byang 
34b4(phal chen): chub sems dpa' bag yod pa la gnas pa yongasu dag par 'gyuro//kye rgyal ba'i sras de la byang chub sems dpa' bag yod /brtson 'grus brtsams pha/dran pa nye bar gzhag pa/mos pa che ba/
34b5(phal chen): rnal 'byor rgyun ma chad pa/chos thams cad la mi gnas pa/chos thams cad la brtson pa/nyon mongs pa med pa'i tshul la zhugs pa/sems yangs pa/sangs rgyas kyi chos 
34b6(phal chen): mtha' dang dbus med pa'i rjesu song ba rnams kyis ni/de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rnams mnyes par 'gyur te/kye rgyal ba'i sras byang chub sems dpas/
34b7(phal chen): chos bcus de bzhin gshegs pa rnams mnyes par bya'o//bcu gang zhe na/byang chub sems dpa'i brtson 'grus thams cad la phyir mi ldog pa dang /lus dang srog la mi lta ba dang /'tsho ba'i yo byad 
34b8(phal chen): dang /'tsho ba'i rkyen la don du gnyer ba med pa dang /chos thams cad rnam par sgom pas/namakha' dang mnyam pa dang /chos kyi dbyings la yang dag par gzhol ba'i lhag mthong la mkhas pa dang /
35a1(phal chen): @#//mi gnas pa'i chos rnam par bsgom pa'i phyag rgya dang /rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par bsgrubs pa'i smon lam chen po dang /yongasu sbyangs pa'i bzod pa shes pa'i snang ba dang /rang gi dge ba'i chos 
35a2(phal chen): bsags pa dang /ma bsags pa shin tu brtags pa dang /mngon par 'du bya ba med pa'i sgo shin tu byang ba ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras bcu po de dag gis de bzhin gshegs pa mnyes par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras de la byang
35a3(phal chen): chub sems dpa' chos bcu la gnas pa rnams kyis ni/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams mnyes par 'gyur ba yin te/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/bag yod pa la gnas pa dang /mi skye ba'i bzod pa la gnas pa dang 
35a4(phal chen): snying rje chen po la gnas pa dang /pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu rdzogs par bya ba dang /spyod pa yongasu rdzogs par bya la gnas pa dang /smon lam mngon par bsgrub pa la gnas pa dang /thabs mkhas pa la gnas pa

35a6(phal chen): pa'i byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis ni/de bzhin bshegs pa mnyes par 'gyuro//kye rgyal ba'i sras de chos bcu dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa'-i rnams ni/sa rnam par dgod pa las 'da' ba yin te/
35a7(phal chen): bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste /bsod nams dang ye shes kyi tshogs shin tu bsags pa dang /pha rol tu phyi pa rnam par phye ba tshad med pa'i lam gzhan gyi dring mi 'jog pa'i ye shes snang ba dang /dge ba'i bshes 
35a8(phal chen): gnyen dang 'grogs pa rgyun ma chad pa dang /brtson 'grus zhig pa med pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis rlabs kyis legs par brlabs pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba sbyor bas mi skyo ba dang /theg 
35b1(phal chen): pa'i rgyan shin tu bshams pa dang /ye shes kyi bsam pa rab tu rno ba dang /sa dang sa ma yin pa'i chos kyi sgo la mi gnas pa dang /'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thams cad dang

35b3(phal chen): dag ni/thog ma nas rnam par dgod pa thams cad la mkhas pa rnam par dpyod pa yin te/chos kyi sgo gang gis gang khong du chud par 'gyur ba dang /ye shes kyi sgo gang gis thob par 'gyur ba dang /sgrub pa gang yin pa dang /
35b4(phal chen): rgyu 'thun pa gang yin pa dang /spyod yul gang yin pa dang /rnam par 'phrul pa gang yin pa dang /yang dag par mthong ba gang yin pa dang /chos kyi sgo rnam par dbye ba'i khyad par gang yin pa dang /ji ltar thob par bya ba thams cad kyang rnam par
35b5(phal chen): dpyod do//de dag la shin tu rnam par dpyad pas/chos thams cad la mi gnas shing /sems kyi rang bzhin gyis chos thams cad de khong du chud pas/byang chub sems dpa'i sa yang dag par 'da'o/
35b6(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras/byang chung sems dpa' rnams ni/'di ltar bdag cag gis ci nas kyang myur du 'da' bar bya'o//snyam du mi sems te/de ci'i phyir zhe na/'di ltar de dag 'di snyam du sems te/bdag cag sa nas
35b7(phal chen): sa la rnam par gnas pas/ji lta ji ltar yang dag par gnas pa de lta de ltar bsod nams kyi tshogs ches che ba skye bar 'gyur te/ji lta ji ltar bsod nams kyi tshogasachen po skyes par gyur pa/de lta de ltar de bzhin
35b8(phal chen): gshegs pa'i sa'i rjesu song zhing sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa tshang med pas nye bar gnas par 'gyuro snyam ste/de dag sbyor ba rgyun ma chad cing /skyo ba med pa'i yid dang ldan pa dang /tshogs kyi rgyan chen pos byang chub sems
36a1(phal chen): @#//dpa'i sa rnam par dgod pa las yang dag par 'da'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras de la byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis rnam pa bcus spyod pa'i dkyil 'khor yongasu sbyong bar byas te/bcu gang zhe na/yo byad yongasu
36a2(phal chen): gzung ba med pa dang /'gro ba thams cad tshim par byed pa dang /tshul khrims yang dag par blangs pa kha na ma tho ba med pa dang /bzod pa dang des pa mi zad pa'i sa'i rjesu song ba dang /brtson 'grus rtsom pa/phyir mi 
36a3(phal chen): ldog pa'i sbyor ba dang ldan pa dang /dran pa'i stongs kyi shugs rmongs pa med pa dang /gcig las rnam pa tha dad pa'i chos kyi rjesu song zhing khong du chud pa dang /gnas med pa'i spyod pa bsgrub dka' ba thams cad la
36a4(phal chen): sems ri'i rgyal po lta bu dang /sems can thams cad dang ma 'dres ba'i mu stegasu gyur pa dang /'gro ba thams chad du sangs rgyas thams cad dang rgyud kyi tshul gcig tu gyur pa ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras

36a7(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi brlabs pa dag par gzung ba la mkhas pa dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen la yang dag par brten pa dang /mos pa dang /brtson 'grus dang /bag yod pa la rab tu gnas pa dang /cho tha dad pa thams 
36a8(phal chen): cad la tha mi dad par rtogs pa dang /snying rje'i byin gyis brlabs shin tu che ba dang /chos thams cad yang dag par ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par lta ba'i shes rab kyis pha rol tu phyin pa mngon par bsgrub pa dang /thabs 
36b1(phal chen): la mkhas pa rnam par dpyod pa'i sbyor ba dang /sbyor ba thams cad la de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs kyi rjesu 'brang ba dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras/rnam pa bcu po de dag gis/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyis spyod pa'i 
36b2(phal chen): dkyil 'khor yongasu sbyangs par bya'o//kye rgyal ba'i sras de la byang chub sems dpa'i smon lam rnam par dag pa bcu ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/skyo ba med par sems can gyi khams yongasu smin par bya ba'i 
36b3(phal chen): smon lam dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba sna tshogs bsags pas/'jig rten gyi khams yongasu dag par bya ba'i smon lam dang /lus dang srog gis bsngo zhing dam pa'i chos yongasu 'dzin pa'i smon lam dang /ye shes kyi sgo 
36b4(phal chen): rnam par dpyad pa khong du chud pas/sangs rgyas kyi zhing la 'jug pa'i smon lam dang /byang chub sems dpa' thams cad dang gnyisu med pas/tshul gcig gi rgyud du 'gro ba'i smon lam dang /de bzhin gshe
36b5(phal chen): pa'i sgo la 'jug pas chos thams cad khong du chud pa'i smon lam dang /mos pa rnam par sbyangs pas blta na don yod pa'i smon lam dang /phyi ma'i mthar byin gyis brlabs pas bskal par gnas pa'i 
36b6(phal chen): smon lam dang /byang chub kyi sems dpa' kun tu bzang po'i theg pa'i rgyan rnam pa thams cad kyi sgo nas/rnam par sbyangs pa'i smon lam dag ste/kye rgyal ba'i sras/byang chub sems dpa' rnams ni de ltar smon 
36b7(phal chen): lam rnam par sbyong ngo//de dag rnam pa bcus smon lam yongasu rdzogs par byed/bcu gang zhe na/yongasu skyo ba med pa dang /rgya chen po mngon par bsgrub pa dang /byang chub sems dad pa' smon lam khyad par du 'phags 
36b8(phal chen): pa rnams kyi rjesu 'jug pa dang /'jig rten gyi khams gzhan na bzhugs pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing thos nas/rang gi smon lam du mngon par bsgrub pa dang/bsam pa phyi ma'i mtha'i mur thug 
37a1(phal chen): @#//pa dang /sems can gyi khams thams cad yongs su smin par bya bar gtugs pa'i smon lam chen po dang ldan pa dang /bskal pa thams cad gnas pas mi 'jigs pa dang /sdug bsngal thams 
37a2(phal chen): cad kyis ji mi snyam pa dang /bde ba thams cad la ye mi brngam pa dang /bsam pa mnyam pa med pa'i rnam par thar pa yongs su srung ba dag ste/de dag smon lam chen po de ltar yongs su sbyong zhing yongs su rdzogs par byed pa'i tshe/
37a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas mthong ba'i gter zad mi shes pa'ang rab tu 'thob bo//gzungs 'khrul pa med pa'i gter zad mi shes pa'anang rab tu 'thob bo//smon lam zad mi shes pas chos thams cad shin tu rtogs pa'i gzungs 
37a4(phal chen): kyang rab tu 'thob bo//snying rje chen pos 'gro ba thams cad yongs su skyob pa'i gter zad mi shes pa 'ang rab tu 'thob bo//ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyud kyi gter zad mi shes pa'ad rab tu 'thob bo//'gro ba thams cad 
37a5(phal chen): tshim par bya ba'i bsod nams kyi gter zad mi shes pa 'anang rab tu 'thob bo/chos thams cad yang dag pa ji lta ba bzhin du brtag pa'i ye shes kyi gter zad mi shes pa'i rab tu 'thob bo//rnam par 
37a6(phal chen): smin pa la gnas pa mngon par bsgrub pa'i gter zad mi shes ba'ang rab tu 'thob bo/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs mtha' yas pa'i gter zad mi shes pas rab tu 'thob bo//'jig rten gyi khams mtha' dang 
37a7(phal chen): dbus med par gtogs pa la 'jug pa'i gter zad mi shes pa'ang rab tu 'thob bo/de ltar gter rab tu thob cing bsod nams dang /ye shes kyi tshogs zad mi shes pa dang ldan pa de dag ye shes chen po'i dkyil 
37a8(phal chen): 'khor yongs su dag pas/sems can rnams la bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du chos kyi gter gyi sbyin pa byedo//de la rgyal ba'i sras/byang chub sems dpa' rnams sems can rnams kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin 
37b1(phal chen): ji ltar 'jug ce na/sbyor ba ji lta bur rgyu ba dang /rgyu dang rkyen ji lta ba dang /sems dang sems las byung ba'i sbyor ba ji lta ba las sems can rnams kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du 'jug ste/de dag de ltar bsam pa la
37b2(phal chen): zhugs pa rnams sems can rnams kyis bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin shes shing /chos yang dag par ston te/de dag 'dod chags kyi bsam pa rnam par bzlog pa'i phyir/mi sdug pa yang dag par ston to//zhe sdang gi bsam
37b3(phal chen): pa rnam par bzlog pa'i phir/byams pa chen po'i sbyor ba yang dag par ston to//gti mug gi bsam pa rnam par bzlo pa'i phyir/chos rnam par 'byed pa la mkhas par yang dag par ston to/cha mnyam pa rnam par bzlog pa 'i
37b4(phal chen): phyir/ye shes kyi khyad par dang ldan pa'i chos kyi sgo yang dag par ston to/zhum pa'i bsam pa rnam par bzlos pa'i phyir/rgyan chen po'i yul yang dag par ston to/bdag gi nga rgyal gyis bcom pa'i
37b5(phal chen): bsam pa rnam par bzlogapa'i phyir/chos kyi mnyam pa nyid yang dag par ston to/g.yo dang sgyus mi mnyam pa'i bsam pa rnam parabazlog pa'i phyir/byang chub sems dpa'i bsam pa mnyam pa nyid dang /zhi ba'i
37b6(phal chen): gnas kyi bsam pa mngon par bsgrub pa yang dag par ston tode dag ni de ltar sems can rnams la bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du chos kyi gter yang dag par ston te/de yang don dbyer med pa yang dag par ston to//yi
37b7(phal chen): ge'i mtshams sbyor bas sbyar bas yang dag par ston toshes pa rnam par dbye bas yang dag par stonatobastan pa snga phyi ma 'dres pas yang dag par ston tochos kyi tshul dang tshul ma yin ba'i bye brag dbyer med
38a1(phal chen): @#//pas yang dag par ston to//mtshan nyid kyi phyag rgya rnam par mi 'khrugs pas yang dag par ston to //chos rab tu dgod pa rgyun chad pa med pas yang dag par ston te//mtha' yas pa 
38a2(phal chen): sbyang pas yang dag par ston no//the tsom thams cad shin tu gcad pa'i phyir yang dag par ston to/dbang po'i rgyud la 'jug pa'i phyir yang dag par sto to//de bzhin gshegs pa'i phyag rgya la dgod par gzhol bas yang 
38a3(phal chen): dag par ston to//yang dag pa'i mtha' mtshan nyid med pa rnam par mi 'khrugs pas sa yang dag par ston to//chos kyi dbyings la yang dag par gzhol bas yang dag par ston to/
38a4(phal chen): /chos kyi rnga yang dag par brdungs pas yang dag par stonato//gnas thams cad yang dag par spang bas yang dag par stonato//de bzhin gshegs pa thams cad rjesu dran pa mi gtong bas yang dag par ston ta//tshig dang skad dang sgra dang ngag gi lam thams cad 
38a5(phal chen): mnyam pa nyid la mi gnas pas yang dag par ston to //rnam par mi rtog pa'i dbyings kyi rjesu 'gro bas yang dag par ston te//dpe thams cad rnam par dgod 
38a6(phal chen): pas rgyud dang mi 'gal bar brtan par bya bas yang dag par stonato //de bzhin gshegs pa thams chad kyi ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor rang gi lus su sems pa la yang dag par gzhol pas yang dag par ston te //de ltar seMn 
38a7(phal chen): la chos yang dag par ston cing rtag tu rgyun mi 'chad par chos kyi nan tan gyi mtshan nyid kyi rjesu 'brang ba dang /rang gi don rting tshugs par byed pa dang /pha rol tu phyin pa'i phyogs mi gtong pas rgyan chen po'i pha rol tu 
38a8(phal chen): phyin pa'i theg pa yang dag par sgrub po/de la'ang sems can thams cad tshim par bya ba dang /yo byad kyis yongasu bstud pa dang /sbyor ba mnyam par yongasu gtong pas/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu dag par byedo//
38b1(phal chen): yang dag par blangs pa mi gtong ba dang /gnas med pa dang /nga rgyal med pas tshul khrims kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu dag par byedo//nyes pa thams cad khyad gsod pa dang /sems can thams cad kyis mi 'khrugs pa
38b2(phal chen): dang /mnyam pa nyid la gzhol ba dang /mthon dman med pa dang /zad mi shes pa'i sa mnyam par 'dzin pas/bzod pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongs dag par byedo//brtsam pa chen por rtsom pa dang /brtsam pa thams cad
38b3(phal chen): la zhum pa med pa dang /sbyor ba phyir mi ldog pa dang /yid la byed pa phyir mi 'gyur ba dang /mthu mtha' yas pa dang /stobs la thub pa med pa dang /sngon du 'dor ba mi 'phrog pa dang /dad pa'i mthu chen po dang /blang
38b4(phal chen): ba dang dor ba med pa'i tshogs thams cad yongasu rgyas par bya ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i sgo nyams pa med pa la yang dag par 'jug pas/brtson 'grus kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yod su dag par
38b5(phal chen): byed do//'dod pa'i yon tan thams cad kyi 'du 'dzi yongasu sbyong ba dang mi 'bral ba dang /rim gyis bsam gtan dang /tinge 'dzin mngon par bsgrub pa dang /gnas med cing blang ba med pa shin tu
38b6(phal chen): rtogs pa dang /nyon mongs pa thams cad rab tu sel ba dang /tinge 'dzin sgo mtha' yas pa mngon par bsgrub pa dang/mngon par shes pa chen pos rnam par 'phrul ba'i rjesu 'gro ba dang /rim gyis rnam par spong ba'i
38b7(phal chen): tinge 'dzin gyi sgo la rab tu 'jug pa dang /tinge 'dzin gcig la sgo mtha' yas pa rab tu 'jug pa dang /ting nge 'dzin gyi spyod yul thams cad shes pa dang /de'i yul gyi rjesu rab tu zhugs pa dang /tinge 'dzin dang
38b8(phal chen): snyoms par 'jug pa thams cad ye shes kyi phyag rgya mi 'khrugs pa'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i sa la nye bar gyur pas/bsam gtan gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu dag par byedo//sangs rgyas kyi chos thams cad
39a1(phal chen): @#//mnyan pa dang /gzung ba dang /blang ba dang /bcang ba dang /dge ba'i bshes gnyen thams cad bsnyen bkur bas yongasu mi skyo ba dang /thos pa thams cad kyis mi ngoms pa dang /ji ltar thos
39a2(phal chen): pa la tshul bzhin du rtog pa dang /de la mngon sum du mnyam par gzhog pa dang /sgro 'dogs pa'i lta ba thams cad dang bral ba dang /chos thams cad rnam par 'byed pa la mkhas pa dang /mtshan nyid kyi phyag rgya tha mi
39a3(phal chen): dad pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sgo nas/lhun gyis grub par sgom zhing /rnam par sgom pa dang /stobs la 'jug pa dang /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes la 'jug pa rgyun mi 'chad pa dang /shes rab kyi
39a4(phal chen): pha rol tu phyin pa sgo kun dang ldan pas/thams cad mkhyen pa'i sgor 'jug pas/shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu dag par byedo//'jig rten gyi bya ba'i rgyud thams cad ston pa dang /sems can gyi
39a5(phal chen): yongasu smin pas yongasu mi skyo ba dang /bsam pa bzhin du bya ba ston pa dang /bya ba thams cad la mngon par chags pa med pa dang /byis pa'i spyod pa dang /'phags pa'i spyod pa bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin
39a6(phal chen): du ston pa dang /'khor ba dang mya ngan las 'das pa nye bar ston pa dang /sbyor ba thams cad shin tu brtags pas rgyu ba dang /rgyan thams cad bstan pa gnas med pa dang /'gro ba thams cad kyi rjesu 'gro zhing rjesu
39a7(phal chen): 'brang ba dang /sems can thams cad sgrol ba'i spyod pas/thabs mkhas pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa yongs su spyod do//sems can gyi khams mthar thugs par yongasu smin par bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi khams
39a8(phal chen): mthar thug par yongasu sbyang pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mthar thug par mchod pa dang /chos chags pa med pa'i mthar thug par rnam par 'tshang rgya ba dang /chos kyi dbyings kyis mthar thugs pa'i spyod pa'i
39b1(phal chen): dkyil 'khor yongasu rdzogs par bya ba dang /bskal pa phyi ma'i mthar thug par rab tu gnas pa dang /sems bskyed pa'i mthar thug par stobs la 'jug pa dang /'gro ba dang ldog pa mthar thug par mngon par 'tshang ba dang /
39b2(phal chen): zhing gi gnas kyi mthar thug par thams cad mthong ba dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes kyi mthar thug pa rab tu thob par bya bas/smon lam gyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu dag par byedo//bsam pa'i stobs kun nas nyon
39b3(phal chen): mongs pa med pa dang /lhag pa'i bsam pa'i stobs dbang du gyur cing thub pa med pa dang /snying rje chen po'i stobs skyo ba med pa dang /byams pa chen po'i stobs snyoms pa'i sbyor ba can dang /gzungs kyi stobs don thams cad
39b4(phal chen): kyi sbyor ba la bsgribs pa med pa dang /spobs pa'i stobs 'gro ba thams cad tshim par byed pa dang /pha rol tu phyin pa'i stobs theg pa thams cad kyi rgyan byed pa dang /smon lam gyi stobs gcadu mi rung ba dang /mngon
39b5(phal chen): par shes pa'i stobs tshad med pa mngon par bsgrub pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs la mos pa dam pa'i stobs rab tu 'dzin pas/stobs kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yongasu dog par byedo//
39b6(phal chen): 'dod chags kyi spyod pa khong du chud pa dang /zhe sdang gi spyod pa rjesu rtogs pa dang /gti mug gi spyod pa rab tu rtogs pa dang /cha mnyam pa'i spyod pa rjesu shes pa dang /slob pa'i sems can gyi spyod pa rab tu rtogs pa 
39b7(phal chen): skad cig gcig gis sems can mtha' yas pa'i spyod pa rnam par rtog pa dang /sems dang bsam pa mtha' yas pa thams cad shes par byed pa dang /chos thams cad la yang dag par ji lta ba bzhin du rtogs
39b8(phal chen): pa dang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs kyi rjesu 'jug pa dang /chos kyi dbyings kyi sgo thams cad dang ldan pa khong du chud pas/ye shes kyi pha rol tu phyin pa yogasu dag par byed do////
40a1(phal chen): @#//bam po nyi shu lnga pa//de dag de ltar pha rol tu phyin pa rnams yongasu sbyong zhing /pha rol tu phyin pa de dag yongasu rdzogs par byed pa dang /pha rol gyi phyogs mi gtong pa dang /byang chub sems 
40a2(phal chen): dpa'i theg pa rgyan chen po'i rgyan dang ldan pa la gnas shing sems can gyi khams ji ltar dam bcas pa bzhin du yang dag par sgrol lo//de dag dge ba'i las shin tu bsags pa ston pas ngan song du 'gro ba'i sems can sgrol;;
40a3(phal chen): brtsam pa chen po la gzud pas/sems can gyi khams mi khom par 'gro ba sgrol lo//lhag mthong nye bar bltabs pas/gzugs kyi khams na 'khod pa'i sems can gyi khams sgrol lo//phra ba'i ye shes nye bar 
40a4(phal chen): bstabs pas/gzugs med pa'i khamasu skyes pa'i sems can rnams sgrol lo//zhi ba'i tshul nye bar bstabs pas/nyan thos dang /rang sangs rgyas kyi theg pa la mos pa'i sems can gyi khams sgo la lo//
40a5(phal chen): stobs bcu'i rgyan chen po dang ldan pa bstan pas/theg pa chen po la mos pa'i sems can kyi khams sgrol lo//de ltar na sngon la sa dang po pa'i sa la gnas pa'i tshe/sems can g.yang sa chen po nas 
40a6(phal chen): lhung pa rnam pa tha dad par gyur pa ji lta ba bzhin du shes te sgo sna tshogs nas gzud par bya'o zhes seng+ge'i sgra bsgrags pa de bzhin du/ye shes thob pa'i rnams sems can thams cad bsgral ba'i phyir rab tu zhugs pa yin no//
40a7(phal chen): kye rgyal ba'i sras/byang chub sems dpa' ye shes de lta bu la zhugs pa ni/rtag tu rgyun mi gcod par sangs rgyas kyi rigs dang /chos kyi rigs dang /dge 'dun gyi rigs mi gcod pa'i phyir zhugs pa yin te/
40a8(phal chen): de dag byang chub kyi sems gzhan gyi sems kyi rgyud la gzhog pas/sangs rgyas kyi rigs mi gcod pa yin/chos kyi mdzod rnam par 'grel p+sa/chos kyi rigs mi gcod pa yi bstan pa'i tshul dang 'gal bar 
40b1(phal chen): mi byed pas/dge 'dun gyi rigs mi gcod pa yin/smon lam chen po yang dag par brjod pas/sangs rgyas kyi rigs mi gcod pa yin/rten cing 'brel par 'byung ba'i tshul yang dag par ston pas/chos kyi rigs mi gcod pa
40b2(phal chen): yin/snying po thob par byed pa'i chos la brtson pas/dge 'dun gyi rigasami gcod pa yin/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sa bon sems can gyi zhing la 'debs pas/sangs rgyas kyi rig mi gcod pa yin/dam pa'i
40b3(phal chen): chos yongasu 'dzin pas/chos kyi rigs migacod pa yin/tshogs chen po yongasu sdud pa mi skyo bas/dge 'dun gyi rigs ma gcod pa yin/'das pa dang /ma byon pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das
40b4(phal chen): rnams kyi mdzad pa dran pa'i phyir/rtag tu de bzhin gshegs pa'i gad mas dag dang rjesu bstan pa mi gtong bas/sangs rgyas dang /chos dang dge 'dun gyi rigs mi gachod pa yin/de dag de ltar skyabs
40b5(phal chen): gsum gyi rigs mi gcad pa'i phyir zhugs pas rtsom pa thams cad la kha na ma tho ba med pa yin te/brtsam pa gang ci rtsom yang rung de dag thams cadu thogs pa med par thams cad mkhyen pa'i sgor
40b6(phal chen): 'byung ba thob par byedo//de dag de ltar lus kyi las kha na ma tho ba med pa dang /ngag gi las kha na ma tho ba medapa dang /yid kyi las kha na ma tho ba mad pa la gnas pas/dge ba'i rtsa ba rtsom pa don yod par rtsom pa yin te/
40b7(phal chen): sems can gyi don sbyor ba don yod par rtsom mo//sems can don yod par 'dul bar byedo//chos don yod par ston to//de'i chos stan pa thams cad kyang sems can gyi bsam pa tshim par bya ba la thog pa med
40b8(phal chen): pas khong du chud par 'gyuro//de'i sems bskyed pa gcig tsam yang log par mi 'gyur te/sems bskyed pa gzhan thabs dang shes rab kyi yongasu zin pa thams cad kyang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyidu yongasu bsngo'o
41a1(phal chen): @#//de de ltar chos dkar po bsags pa don yod pa de lta bu la gnas pas/rgyan rnam par phye ba bcus sems bskyed pa thams cad yongasu bsngo ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di ltar lus kyi rgyan sems can 
41a2(phal chen): 'dul ba ji bzhin du yang dag par ston pa dang /ngag gi rgyan the tsom thams cad gcod pa dang /sems kyi rgyan skad cig gcig gis ting 'dzin rjesu rtogs par byed pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rgyan kun nas nyon mongs
41a3(phal chen): pa med pa dang /'od kyi rgyan 'od zer gyi dra ba mtha' dang dbus med pa bkye bas/'gro ba thams cad snang bar byed pa dang /'khor gyi dkyil 'khor gyi rgyan sems can gyi rnam pa tshad med pa'i tshogs yongasu mgu bar byed
41a4(phal chen): ba dang /rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul ba'i rgyan sems can gyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du/rdzu 'phrul gyi rnam par 'phrul ba dang /bstan pa'i rgyan mkhas pa'i skye bo thams cad kyis yongasu gzung ba dang /mya ngan 
41a5(phal chen): las 'das pa'i rgyan mngon par sangs rgyas pa gcig gis phyogs bcu ma lus par rgyas par 'gengs pa dang /chos byin gyi sa brlab pa'i rgyan yul dang dus dang /snod ji lta ba bzhin du sdod 
41a6(phal chen): pa dag ste/de dag rgyan de lta bus sems bskyed pa thams cad mngon par bsgrub pas/lus dang ngag dang /yid kyi las don yod pa dang /ye shes chen po don yod pa rab tu 'thob ste/de dag mthong bas kyang 'di 
41a7(phal chen): ltar bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yongasu smin par 'gyur ba'i phyir/sems can rnams la don yod ba yin te/thos pa dang /bsnyen bkur ba dang /'grogs pa dang /rjesu dran pa dang /
41a8(phal chen): rjesu rab tu byung ba dang /chos ston ba dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba la rjesu yid rangs pa dang /dad cing mos pa dang /chung ngu na ming brjod pa tsam yang 'di ltar bla na med pa yang dgapar rdzogs pa'i byang chub du yongasu 
41b1(phal chen): smin pa'i phyir/sems can rnams la don du 'gyur ba yino//kye rgyal ba'i sras/'di lta ste dper na/rtsi'i ming mthong na bde ba zhes bya ba de mthong ma thag tu sems can rnams kyi lus la zhen pa 'am/ltor zos pa'i 
41b2(phal chen): dug rab tu zhi bar 'gyuro//kye rgyal ba'i sras/de bzhin du chos de lta bu dang ldan pa'i byang chub sems dpa' yang mthong ma thag tu sems cad rnams kyi nyon mongs pa'i dug thams cad byang bar 'gyur te/chos dkar po'i 
41b3(phal chen): stobs kyi shugs kyang skye bar 'gyuro//kye rgyal pa'i sras/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po chos nyid de lta bu dang ldan zhing chos de dag la gnas te/shes rab kyi 'od chen pos ma rig pa'i mun pa 
41b4(phal chen): bsal nas/bdud kyi dkrugs ma'i 'khor thams cad byams pa dang snying rje chen po'i stobs kyis pham par byas te/ye shes dang bsod nams kyi stobs kyi shugs kyis phas kyi rgol ba thams cad zil 
41b5(phal chen): gyis mnan nas/rdo rje lta bu'i tinge 'dzin la 'jug pas/sems kyi dri ma thams cad dang /nyon mongs pa'i phyogs thams cad bsal nas/skyo ba med pa'i stobs dang shugs kyi dge ba'i rtsa 
41b6(phal chen): ba phun sum tshogs pa thams cad rab tu sgrub ste/sngan chad sangs rgyas kyi zhing yongasu dag par spyad pa'i stobs dang shugs gyis mi khom pa dang /ngan song dang ngan 'gro'i lam thams cad rnam par bzlog nas/mngon par 
41b7(phal chen): zhen pa med pa'i stobs dang shug kyis/ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor yongasu sbyangs te/rim gyi thabs dang shes rab kyi stobs dang shugs kyis byang chub sems dpa'i sa dang pha rol tu phyin pa dang /tinge 'dzin dang 
41b8(phal chen): mngon par shes pa dang /rig pa dang mi 'jigs pa yongasu dag pa mngon par bsgrubs nas/chos dkar po thams cad kyi stobs dang shugs kyi/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi mtshan dang /dpe byad bzang po dang /
42a1(phal chen): @#//sku dang gsung dang thugs kyi rgyan yongasu dag pa mngon par bsgrub pa rab tu rgyas par bya ste/ye shes kyis shes par bya ba'i dbang dang /byin gyis brlabs kyis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs 
42a2(phal chen): dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos ma 'dres pa thams cad mnyam pa nyidu khong du chud par byas nas/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub kyang myur du 'thob cing /thams cad mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi yul yang smon laM
42a3(phal chen): gyi khyad par ji lta ba bzhin du 'thob par 'gyuro//sngon gyi byin gyi rlabs dang /ye shes kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis mngon par sangs rgyas nas/sems can rnams kyi bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du/sangs rgyas kyi zhing stan 
42a4(phal chen): te|'dul ba ji lta ba bzhin du sems can rnams la chos kyi 'khor lo chen po bskor cing mtha' yas pa'i sems can rnam par 'dulo//kye rgyal ba'i sras/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' chos de lta bu dang ldan

42a7(phal chen): mdzod chen po gzung zhing bsrung ba'i phyir/tha dad pa yang dag par shes pas/chos kyi tshul rab tu dbye ba'i rjesu song ba'i byang chub sems dpa'i tshig gis 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor mtha' yas par/bdag gis
42a8(phal chen): lus kyi rgyan byin gyis rlabs te bstan pa mngon par bsgrub pa rnam pa'i mchog thams cad dang ldan pa dang /tha dad pa yang dag parashes pa'i mkhas pa mtha' yas pa dang /ye shes mi zad pa'i sgor 'jug pa dang /dbyangs kyi
42b1(phal chen): dkyil 'khor shin tu rnam par phye ba'i byin gyis rlabs dang /dbyangs gcig bsgrags pas 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor thams cad mgu bar byed pa'i dbyangs kyi yan lag rnam par dag pa bsam pa dang /bag la nyal ba dang /shes 
42b2(phal chen): pa'i sa ji lta ba bzhin du rjesu 'brang ba dang /lus kyi byin gyis rlabs kyi 'khor gyi dkyil 'khor tham sa cad kyi nang du phyin kyang zil gyis mi non pa dang /lus kyi rgyan sgo thams cad rnam par bstan pa dang /'khor gyi dkyil 
42b3(phal chen): 'khor thams cad kyi sems dang /bsam pa khong du chud pas/rab tu rtogs pa dang /tshig grol ba'i byin gyis rlabs kyis bstan pa dang /chos 'chad pa'i byin gyi rlabs la mkhas pas/chos kyi gtam chen po byed de/
42b4(phal chen): stobs dang shugs kyi dbang thub pa med pa dang /'jigs pa med pa'i dbang zhum pa med pa dang /chos kyi dbang zil gyis mi non pa dang/ye shes kyi dbang gzhan gyis mi thub pa dang /shes rab kyi pha rol tu phyin pa'i

43b7(phal chen): byed spyod mchog nyams pa med par byed//thub pa'i smon lam rdzogs byed ston pa'ang de bzhin byed//ye shes gcig la zhugs pa de dag thob bya ba//thob nas 'gro ba kun la sbyin pa rab tu gtong //bsam pa dag
43b8(phal chen): dang khams dang sbyor ba shes byas nas//dbang po ji bzhin mkhas pa de ltar chos kyang ston//pha rol phyin la brtson pa mthu chen ldan pa'i rnams//'gro ba rnams la chos ston bdag gi don mi gtong //pha rol

44a2(phal chen): rigs mi gcod zhugs pa yis//dkar po'i spyod pa gang yang rung ste spyod pa'i rnams//thams cad don yod rgyal ba rnams kyi sar 'gro'o//de dag ci spyod thams cad 'gro ba rnams la yang //rnam smin don yod 
44a3(phal chen): nyon mongs dri ma sbyong bar 'gyur//'di 'dra'i chos ldan de dag mun pa bsal byas shing //bdud 'khrug 'khor lo pham byas byang chub thob par byed//de ltar brtson zhing ye shes thob pa'i bde gshegs sras//bde gshegs

44a6(phal chen): 'gro ba'i rnams//seng ge'i rtsal ltar rtag tu sus kyang mi thub cing //ye shes rgya mtsho lta bu ri rab bzhin gnas pa//sprin ltar 'gro ba kun gyi nyon mongs zhi bar byed//byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi blo gros kyis

44b2(phal chen): pa'i zhabs kyi drung na 'khod par sheso//bcom ldan 'das kyis rdzu 'phrul mngon par 'du mdzad pa ma bshig ma btang bar byang chub kyi shing drung yang ma spangs par ri rab kyi rtse mo nas/mtshe ma'i gnas kyi lha'i bu rab mtshe ma'i

44b6(phal chen): byas pa/bog re brgya stong phrag bcus brgyan pa/gser gyi dra ba brgya stong phrag bcus kun nas mngon par bres pa/me tog gi bla re brgya stong phrag bcus kunatu khyab par byas pa/phreng ba'i bla re brgya stong phrag bcu kun tu rgyas

44b8(phal chen): gdugs brgya stong phrag bcus kunanas bzung ba/rin po che'i gdugs brgya stong phrag bachu kun nas bzung ba/'od zer brgya stong phrag bcus snang bar byas pa/rab mtshe ma'i lha'i rgyal po brgya stong phrag bcus rab tu btud pa/
