46a5(phal chen): kyi bol gnyis nas/'od zer bye ba brgya stong bkye ste/phyogs bcu thams cad snang bar byas nas/'jig rten gyi khams tha mas cad bstan te/byang chub sems dpa' de dag gis/'jig rten 
46a6(phal chen): gyi khams thams cad kyi glingabazhi pa kun gyi rab mtshe ma'i gnas thams cad na yang /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rnam par 'phrul ba yod pa mthong //de nas byang chub sems dpa' yon tan dang ldan pas/sangs rgyas kyi
46a7(phal chen): mthus phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//sangs rgyas 'od zer 'od snang bas//phyogs rnams thams cad rnam par snang //des ni rab tu gsal byas pas//mig la rab rib
46a8(phal chen): gang yang med//mtshe ma rab kyi gnas 'di na//gang zag dam pa sangs rgyas bzhugs//de bzhin kun na 'ang gsal bar snang //de ni 'jig rten ngo mtshar to//sems can gtso bo bcu'i rnams//gshegs par
46b1(phal chen): gyur pas mngon par bstod//de dag 'khor kyang ci 'dra ba//de bzhin du kun na rab tu snang //phyogs rnams bcu po thams cad na//chos kyi dam pa rab tu ston//de kun ming yang de bzhin te//bdag cag rnams kyi kun rdzob
46b2(phal chen): bzhin//'jig rten khams kyi ming dag kyang //de zhes bya ba kun nas 'ongs//gang zag dam pa de dag ni//tshangs pa'i spyod pa'ang de na spyod//bdag cag rnams la gang btags pa//kun gyi ming yang de dang 'thun//
46b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas zhing rnams 'thun ldan pa//de dag sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul pa'o//mi'i 'jig rten kun na 'ang //'di na rgyal ba bzhugs par shes//gzhan gyis de'i rgyal po yi//gnas na de bzhin bzhugs pa mthong
46b4(phal chen): ngo//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad na//gang zag dam pa de bzhugs kyang //rgyal ba mtshe ma rab gnas na//bzhugs par bdag cag kun kyang sems//'jig rten khams rnam thams cad na//
46b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas gzi brjid bsam mi khyab//de dag smon lam bskul ba'i phyir//'jig rten khamasu rab tu bkye//sems can gtso bo'i rnam 'phrul ba//'di dag de rnams kun gyis mthong //sangs
46b6(phal chen): yon tan mtha' yas te//'jig rten zang zing thub pas spangs//phyogs rnams thams cad kun tu rgyu//chags pa med pa nam mkha' bzhin//de ni gcig las shin tu 'das//mang po dag tu 'ang mi dmigaso//mtha'
46b7(phal chen): yas legs mnga' sangs rgyas rnams//de dag shes par ji ltar rung //gnas pa med cing len pa med//chos dbyings rtogs par thugasu chud//de nas byang chub sems dpa' blo gros dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas
46b8(phal chen): kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas ste//de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//'jig rten mgon po 'phrul chen rnams//rdul med gang zag dam pa dag/'dus shing tshogs pa bsam yas pa'i//
47a1(phal chen): @#//bskal pa dag tu shin tu dkon//de dag thams cad kun gzigs pa//sems can gtso bo 'od mdzade//srog chags kun la phan pa'i phyir//sems can rnams la chos ston to//'jig rten
47a2(phal chen): mun nag 'di dag tu//'jig rten mgon po byung gyur pa//'jig rten sgron ma de dag ni//blta bar shin tu dka' ba yin//sbyin pa tshul khrims bzod pa'i rab//brtson 'grus bsam gtan bsgom pa dang //shes rab sngan
47a3(phal chen): chad spyad pas na//'gro ba thams cad snang bar mdzad//sangs rgyas 'dra ba gang yang med//de phyir de dang mnyam pa med//chos rnams yod pa ma yin pas//skyes bu'i dam pa mthong ba med//sangs rgyas shes rab chen
47a4(phal chen): po rnams//mtshan gyi tshogs ni bsam mi khyab//sku'i tshogs kyis shin tu 'phags//chos rnams kun gyis yang dag byung //'jig rten grags chen dag pa'i rnams//dpa' ba chen po de dag snang //de dag
47a5(phal chen): mtshan tsam thos pas kyang //ngan song thams cad spangs par 'gyur//bskal pa rnams ni brjod med par//byang chub don du 'khor byas kyang //'di'i tshul ni ma shes na//byang chub don du zhugs
47a6(phal chen): pa min//sangs rgyas bsam gyis mi khyab pa//bskal pa bye bar bkur ba dang //gang gis tshul 'di bsam pa gnyis//'di ni de las khyad 'phags//gang gis brjod yas zhing gi rnams//rin chen gang ste thub
47a7(phal chen): gsol kyang //'di'i tshul ni mi shes na//sangs rgyas byang chub de la ring //de nas byang chub sems dpa' dpal dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyis byin gyi rlabs kyis//phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe
47a8(phal chen): tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//nam mkha' dri ma med pa la//nyi ma'i 'od zer mtha' yas pa//dpyid kyi zla ba tha ma la//phyogs bcu dag tu rab tu snang //de tshe smig rgyu ci 'dra ba//de dag bgrang bar mi nus te//mig
47b1(phal chen): can rnams kyang de la rmongs//long pa rnams kyi spyod yul min//de bzhin sems can gtso bo yi//yon tan mtha' dang dbus med pa//bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par//sus kyang shes par mi nuso//de dag gang nas 
47b2(phal chen): blangs pa med//de bzhin sus kyang byas pa med//de ni ci las skyes pa med//rnam par bsgoms na yod pa min//'ongs pa med pa'i chos rnams la//'gro ba de bzhin mi dmigs te//de phyir de dag rgyu yang med//de bzhin
47b3(phal chen): 'jig pa'ang yod pa min//'jig pa med pa'i chos rnams kyi//rang bzhin gang gis shes pa ni//gang la skye ba med pa yi//yang dag sangs rgyas mthong ba min//skyes pa med pa'i chos rnams kyi //rang bzhin dngos 
47b4(phal chen): po mi dmigs te//de 'dri'i don du shes pas ni//dmigs pa med pa shes pa yin//'di la shes byed gang yang med//chos rnams thams cad mtshan nyid med//chos nyid 'di ni shes gyur na//yongasu shes pa gang yang 
47b5(phal chen): med//skye ba dag ni brjod pa ste//de phyir zhing rnams rab tu brjod//zhing gi rang bzhin shes gyur na//'khrug pa med pa rab tu shes//gang tshe 'jig rten zhing yin par//tshul bzhin du ni rtog 
47b6(phal chen): byed na//de tshe thams cad don bstan pa//byang chub sems dpas rab tu shes//de nas byang chub sems dpa' dga' ba dang ldan pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus phyogs bcur rnam par bltas ste//de'i tshe tshigasu bcad 
47b7(phal chen): pa 'di dag smraso//gnas 'di shin tu rgya che ste//chos kyi dbyings kyis klas pa yin//rtag tu kun tu song ba ste//yidu 'ong zhing gnas kyang med//de la yang dag brtag byas shing //rim gro yod par dad byed na//
47b8(phal chen): sdug bsngal nam yang mi mthong ste//ngan song rnams kyang mi mthong ngo //tshad med de bzhin grangs med pa'i//'jig rten khamasu su 'gro bas//gang rnams 'di na snang ba yi//sangs rgyas byin rlabs thos par 'gyur//
48a1(phal chen): @#//drang srong chen po'i byin gyi rlabs//gang gis thos par gyur nas kyang //'di 'dra sangs rgyas chos zhes//rtog med bla med byang chubo//gang rnams 'das pa'i sangs rgyas kyis//chos nyid la ni 
48a2(phal chen): yid ces pa//de kun 'jig rten sgron ma ste//rkang gnyis ldan pa'i dam par 'gyur//sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul ba 'di//la la zhig gis thos gyur cing //thos nas dad par byed 'gyur na//de dag mi'i dam par 
48a3(phal chen): 'gyur//brtan pa gang rnams da ltar yang //sangs rgyas chos la dad byed pa//de dag don gzigs mi 'jigs pa'i//de bzhin gshegs par 'gyur ba yin//bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par//'di 'dra thos 
48a4(phal chen): pa shin tu dkon//smon lam dbang ni ma gtogs par//blo gros 'di 'drar 'gyur ba min//sangs rgyas dam chos 'di las kyang //legs par bshad pa bzung byas shing //gzhan la ston par su byed pa//
48a5(phal chen): de dag bsam gyis mi khyab 'gyur//de la brtson 'grus bskyed byas shing //yid la go cha bgo byas te//gang las sa ngas rgyas brjod pa yi//shes pa 'di 'dra mnyan par bya//de nas byang 
48a6(phal chen): chub sems dpa' 'khrel dang ldan pas/sangs rgyas kyis byin gyi rlabs kyis/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigs su bcad smras pa/gang zhig thos nas 'di 'dra ba'i//dga' ba yangs pa skye ba dang //
48a7(phal chen): the tsom rnam par 'chad pa ni//'jig rten na ni ngo mtshar che//'di ltar thams cad gzigs pa'i rnams//nyid kyis 'gro la bka' stsal na//sangs rgyas mi mkhyen gang yang med//de ltar sangs rgyas bsam 
48a8(phal chen): mi khyab//mi shes pa yi rgyu las ni//ye shes 'jug par mi 'gyur te//mun pa'i mthu dang snang ba gnyis//lhan cig 'byung ba mi 'gyur bstan//ji ltar gzugs dang gzugs med pa//bskal pa mnyam 
48b1(phal chen): pa med par bshad//de ltar shes dang mi shes pa//'grogs par shin tu mi 'gyuro//'khor dang mya ngan 'das pa dag/ji ltar med las brtags pa ltar//de bzhin shes dang mi shes pa/mtshan nyid bzhin du dmigs
48b2(phal chen): pa med//ji ltar 'jig rten 'jig pa dang //'du bar 'gyur ba ring ba ste//phan tshun 'grogs par mi 'gyur ba//shes dang mi shes de bzhino//ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' yi//thog ma'i sems dang tha ma'i sem+sa//
48b3(phal chen): lhan cig mi gnas byung ba ltar//shes dang mi shes de bzhIno//ji ltar lus dang rnam shes pa//gzhan dang gzhan las 'byung 'gyur ba//de bzhin shes dang mi shos gnyis//'grogs par 'gyur ba nam yang
48b4(phal chen): med//ji ltar dug la phan pa'i rtsi//'phral du dug ni dug min byed//de ltar shes pa mi shes pa//shin tu sel ba byed par brjod//sangs rgyas bla na gang yang med//de dang 'dra ba'ang yod pa min//de las
48b5(phal chen): gzhan du 'gyur ba med//de bas rgyal ba shin tu dkon//de nas byang chub sems dpa' brtson 'grus dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas sa kyi mthus/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcade smras
48b6(phal chen): pa|chos rnams 'di dag 'gal med par//mkhyen nas sangs rgyas thugasu chud//des ni mi mkhyen gang yang med//ye shes kyi 'ang pha rol phyin//ji ltar gser dang sa le sbram//ngo bo nyid kyis 'gal ba med//
48b7(phal chen): de ltar chos min de bzhin chos//de dag thams cad mtshan nyid gcig/sems can sems can ma yin pa//ngo bo nyid kyi rang bzhin med//gang gsu med las byung ba ltar//chos rnams rang bzhin de bzhino//
48b8(phal chen): ma 'ongs dus ni ji lta bur//'das pas brjod par bya ba ste//de la mtshan nyid yod pa min//de bzhin chos la mtshan nyid med//'brel par skye dang 'gog pa dang //rang bzhin med dang rang bzhin dag/
49a1(phal chen): @#//rnam pa tha dad gyur pa ltar//sangs rgyas rang bzhin de bzhino//mya ngan 'das pa gzung med pa//gnyis kyi dus na brjod par byas//de la bye brag gang yang med//de bzhin gzugs la rnams 
49a2(phal chen): rtog pa'o//ji ltar ji zhig bgrang byas shing //bgrang bar byed ces brjod pa'ang //de la tha dad gang yang med//de bzhinagazugs kyi rang bzhino//ji ltar gcig nas gcig gi blar//mang po'i tshul du bgrang ba 
49a3(phal chen): yis;;;dngos po grangs med 'gyur ba'ang //blo'i rnam pa tha dad bstan//'jig rten khams rnams la la dag/bsam gyis mi khyab tshig gyur kyang //namakha' 'jig par 'gyur ba med//rang 'byung ye shes 
49a4(phal chen): de bzhino//ji ltar 'jig rten phyogs bcu yi//namakha' so sor brtag pa ltar//de bzhin sangs rgyas rnam mthong ste//sangs rgyas rnams la rtog mi mnga'//de nas byang chub sems dpa' stobs

49a6(phal chen): sems can de dag thams cad kyang //phung po lnga las shin tu brjod//de rnams las kyi phung chen yin//las kyi rtsa ba'ang sems yin te//sems ni sgyu ma bzhin du bshad//sgyu mar gyur pa 'gro 'di'o//
49a7(phal chen): gang na 'jig rten 'di 'gyur zhing //yang na ldog par 'gyur ba yi//'jig rten 'jig rten mi byed de//gzhan gyi rgyud kyang byas pa min//'jig rten 'di na 'byung ba 'byung //'jig rten 'dir yang 
49a8(phal chen): ldog par 'gyur//'byung zhes brjod par mi bya ste//de ni 'jig rten zhes pa yin//gang la 'jig rten snang ba zhes//'du shes rnam par brtags pa yi//'jig rten zhes bya gang zhig yin//sna ba zhes
49b1(phal chen): bya'ang ci zhig yin//dus gsum dag tu rgyu ba yi//phung po 'di ni 'jig rten yin//de dag 'gog pa snang ba zhes//'gro ba dag tu 'du shes gyur//phung po zhes bya ba 'di ni ji//phung po'i rang bzhin ji lta bu//phung po rnams
49b2(phal chen): ni mi 'jig phyir//dngos po med ces rab tu brjod//phung po 'di dag rnam brtags don//de dag rang bzhin stong pa yin//mi 'jig don ni stong pa ste//dngos po med ces bya ba'o//'jig rten ci bzhin sangs rgyas te//
49b3(phal chen): sangs rgyas chos kyang sangs rgyas rnams//ngo bo nyid kyi rang bzhin med//ma nor yang dag ji bzhin pa'i//chos nyid 'di ni rtogs gyur nas//kun gzigs thams cad mkhyen pa yi//sangs rgyas kun na rab tu snang //
49b4(phal chen): de nas byang chub sems dpa' rig pa dang ldan pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigasu bcade smras pa/rang bzhin dngos po med pa yi//'jig rten khams rnams thams
49b5(phal chen): cad kyi//sa'i khams rnams ci bzhin te//sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul ngo mtshar che//de'i rang bzhin 'di yin la//brjod cing bsngags pa mtha' yas te//'das pa'i 'jig rten khams rnamasu//
49b6(phal chen): sus kyang brjod pa ma yino//gzhan du sems can las kyis na//shin tu bstan par brjod pa ste//sems can rnams ni ma gtogs par//las shes bya ba ci mi dmigs//de bzhin stong pa'i las kyis kyang //
49b7(phal chen): brjod pa rnams ni tshad med pas//sems can kun gyi lus su gyur//de la 'ongs pa gang yang med//ji ltar kha dog dbyibs kyi rnams//las kyi rgyu ni bsam mi khyab//snang yang de dag de na med//rnam par rig pa'i dngos nyid
49b8(phal chen): 'dra//de bzhin sems can gtso bo yi//sku ni bsam gyis mi khyab pa//brjod pa rnams kyang tshad med par//zhing rnams kun na rab tu snang //skus ni rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas min//sangs rgyas rnams la sku mi mnga'//
50a1(phal chen): @#//dpa' bo chen po chos sku ste//chos kun rtogs par thugs su chud//gang gis rang 'byung rnams kyi sku//yang dag ji bzhin mthong ba ni//sangs rgyas chos ni rnam kun dang //chos rnams la'ang rmongs pa med//
50a2(phal chen): 'das par gyur ba'i chos rnams kun//mya ngan 'das par shes gyur na//de dag shin tu mi gnas pa'i//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas mthong ba yin//yang dag sangs rgyas sus mthong zhing //dran pa shin tu bsgom byas te//
50a3(phal chen): mtshan med tha dad med pa'i rnams//de dag chos kyi rgyal po'i sras//de nas byang chub sems dpa' blo dang ldan pas//sangs rgyas kyi mthus/phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigs su bcade smras pa//ji ltar 
50a4(phal chen): ri mo'i las rnams la//tshon rnams sna tshogs kun 'du ba//kun kyang 'byung ba chen por mnyam//sems kyis yongs su brtags pa yin//khams la tshon rtsi de dag med//tshon rtsi la'ang khams med de//khams 
50a5(phal chen): rnams ma gtogs gzhan na yang //tshon ces bya ba gang yang med//sems la ri mo de med de//ri mo la'ang sems medo//sems de ma gtogs gzhin du 'ang //ri mo gang yang mi dmigs so//sems 
50a6(phal chen): de rtag pa ma yin pa//bsam gyis mi khyab tshad med cing //phan tshun shes pa med pa yi//gzugs rnams thams cad ston par byed//ji ltar ri mo mkhan gyi rnams//sems ni gang gis ri mo de//bris pa'i 
50a7(phal chen): sems kyang mi shes pa//de bzhin chos kyi rang bzhino//sems ni ri mo mkhan dang 'dra//sems ni phung po byed pa ste//'jig rten khams na ji snyed pa'i//'jig rten 'di dag sems kyis bris//sems dang 'dra ba 
50a8(phal chen): sangs rgyas te//sangs rgyas ji bzhin 'gro ba'o//sems dang sangs rgyas dag la yang //ngo bo nyid kyis zad pa med//sems kyi rgyud rnams ci 'dra ba//de dag sangs rgyas rab tu mkhyen//de phyir sangs rgyas 
50b1(phal chen): rang bzhin gyis//'gro ba sna tshogs gyur par snang //lus kyi rnams la sems med de//sems la 'ang lus rnams yod pa min//sangs rgyas mdzad pa'ang rab tu byed//de ltar spyod pa ngo mtshar che//gang zhig dus gsuM
50b2(phal chen): thams cad kyi//rgyal ba thams cad shes 'dod na//sangs rgyas thams cad sems kyi dngos//chos kyi dbyingasu blta bar gyis//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ye shes dang ldan pas/sangs rgyas kyi mthus/phyogs

50b5(phal chen): rnams kyang tshim mi 'gyur//de bzhin mtha' yas brjod ldan pa 'i//brjod pa gang zhig smra 'dod pa//bskal pa grangs med zas byas kyang //rang 'byung rnams kyi brjod mi zad//yid bzhin nor bu
50b6(phal chen): dag pa ni//kha dog kun tu rab tu snang //de la kha dog yod pa min//sangs rgyas yon tan de bzhino//ji ltar namakha' dri med cing //gzugs med bstan du med yin yang //gzugs rnams thams cad ston par byed//
50b7(phal chen): namakha' snang ba gang yang med//de bzhin thams cad gzigs pa'i rnams//yon tan kun dang ldan ston kyang //de ni sus kyang mthong ba med//sems la'ang de ni mi gnaso//sangs rgyas sgra ni grags mod kyi//de bzhin
50b8(phal chen): gshegs pa sgra ma yin//sgra ni ma gtogs gzhan du'ang //sangs rgyas shes par mi rung ngo//ji ltar bdag gis mthong tshul du//'di ni shes bya'i gnas ma yin//sangs rgyas byang chub blang
51a1(phal chen): @#//du med//de la rnam par rtog pa med//sangs rgyas chos rnams gang dag kyang //su la'ang sangs rgyas gsung ba med//sems can bsam pa ji bzhin du//de la de 'dra ston pa mdzad//

51a7(phal chen): sems dpa'i tinge 'dzin shinatu rnam par phye ba'i rnam pa 'byed pa la mnyam par bzhag pa legaso legaso//rigs kyi bu khyod la ni de bzhin gshegs pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong prag bcu'i rdul shin tu pra 
51a8(phal chen): ba snyed thams cad kyang mtshan 'thun pa sha stag gis/'di ltar bcom ldan 'das de bzhin gshegs pa rnam par snang mdzad kyi sngon gyi smon lam dang /sngon gyi byin gyi rlabs dang /byang chub sems dpa' 
51b1(phal chen): rnams kyi dge ba'i rtsa bas shin tu brtan pas na/'di ltar chos bstan pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi ye shes rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /'jig rten gyi khams la 'jug par bya ba dang /sems can gyi khaMs rnam par bsgom
51b2(phal chen): pa dang /chags pa med pa la 'jug par bya ba dang/sgribs pa med pa'i spyod yul dang /kun nas rab tu sbyor ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa rab tu bsgrub pa dang /chos thams cad khong du chud par bya ba dang /dbang po thaMd
51b3(phal chen): yongasu shes par bya ba dang /chos bstan pa thams cad yongasu gzung par bya ba dang /'di ltar byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa bcu brtsam pa rab tu 'thib par bya ba'i phyir/byin gyi brlab pa mdzad kyis/rigs kyi bu khyod
51b4(phal chen): kyis sangs rgyas kyi mthu dang /sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis brlabs kyis chos 'di bstan pa la spobs par gyis shig/de nas sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das de dag gis/byang chub sems dpa' yon tan

60a1(phal chen): @#//snyan pa bskal pa grangs med par mi sdod pa/'khrug par byed pa/sems 'khrug pa/lus 'khrug pa/dbang po 'khrug pa/myos par byed pa/'khor bar byed pa/rnyogs par byed pa/rnam par

62a1(phal chen): @#//kyi snang ba bla na med pa la zhugs nas/sems can 'di dag yongasu ma smin cing rnam par ma dul bar bor te/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu mngon par 'tshang rgya ba na dus ma yin

72b8(phal chen): dpa' rnams kyi spyod pa'i chos nyid 'di ston pa legaso legaso//kye rgyal ba'i sras bdag cag kun kyang yon tan dang ldan ba zhes bya ba dag ste/de bzhin gshegs pa'i mtshan kun tu yon tan zhes bya ba dag gi
73a1(phal chen): @#//drung nas/'jig rten gyi khams yon tan gyi rgyal mtshan zhes bya ba dag nas phyin te/thams cad na yang sangs rgyas kyi mthus 'khor 'di 'dra ba dag na/tshig 'di dag dang /yi ge 'di dag dang /bstan pa 'di 
73a2(phal chen): dag dang /don 'di nyid brjod pas/lhag chad med par chos bstan pa 'di ston to//kye rgyal ba'i sras/de ltar bdag cag thams cad kyang /sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs kyis 'khor 'dir phyin te/ji ltar 'khor 'dir bdag 
73a3(phal chen): cag phyin pa de bzhin du/phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams thams cad kyi gling bzhi pa'i 'jig rten gyi khams tham sa cad kyi rab mtshe ma'i gnas lha'i rgyal po rab mtshe ma'i khang pa nor bu sna tshogs shes bya ba kun tu yang /
73a4(phal chen): byang chub sems dpa' sangs rgyas kyi zhing brgya stong rdul shin tu phra ba snyed par gyur to////bam po nyi shu brgyad pa//de nas byang chub sems dpa' yon tan dang ldan pas phyogs bcur bltas te/
73a5(phal chen): 'khor yod pa thams cad la rnam par bltas nas/chos kyi dbyings la rnam par lta zhing /de bzhin gshegs pa'i rigs yang dag par gzung ba dang /byang chub sed de'i rigs yongs sbyang ba dang /smon laM gyi rigs phyir mi ldog pa bya ba dang /spyod pa'i rigs rgyun 
73a6(phal chen): tu bsab pa dang /dus gsuM gyi rgyud mnyaM bar bya ba dang /'das pa dang /mkhyen pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thaMd kyi rigs yang dag par gzungs ba dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba ji ltar bskyed pa'i rgyur brjod pa dang /dus dang ji ltar 'byor ba'i yan lag dang /dbang po dang /bsam pa 
73a7(phal chen): dang /mos pa dang /nyon mongs pa dang /bag chags dang /sems dang /spyod pa dang /rnam pa g.yos pa'i rgyud thams cad yongasu brtag pa dang /sangs rgyas dang /byang chub sems dpa'i rigs thams cad snang bar 
73a8(phal chen): byed cing /sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis/de'i tshe tshigasu bcade smras pa/namakha'i lam gyi rgyud gshegs shing /sgribs pa med par gzigs pa rdul rnams sbyangs gyur pa/kun tu mtha' yas spyod yul can/
73b1(phal chen): stobs bcu mnga' ba de la phyag 'tshalo//gang yang rgyal ba mi'i mchog/chags pa mi mnga' yon tan tshad med mnga' ba ste//'das dus mnyam bas mnyam gyur pa//rdul bstsal de dag spyod pa 'di yino//phyogs bcu'i 'jig
73b2(phal chen): rten khams dag na//don dam pa gzigs pa brtan pa bzhugs pa'i rnams//mtha' yas nyes pa kun bsal ba//gnas med de dag spyod pa 'di yino //chos dbyings bsam gyis mi khyab par//skyes bu'i seng ge gang
73b3(phal chen): rnams byon par ma gyur pa//thugs rje che bskyed rnam spyod pa//phan mdzad de dag spyod pa 'di yi no//dus gsum gzhal du med pa yi//rang byung gti mug rnam par bsal ba ji snyed pa//thams cad chos nyid mnyam ba ste//
73b4(phal chen): stobs mnga' de dag spyod pa 'di yin no//gang rnams mtha' dbus med pa yi//'jig rten khams kyi srid pa'i rgyud rnams rab gzigs shing //gzigs nas rtog pa mi mnga' ba//mi g.yo de dag spyod pa 'di yi'o//
73b5(phal chen): gang rnams chos kyi dbyings mkhyen cing //rnam dag don gyi dam pa gzigs pa brtan rnams//gti mug tha ba nga rgyal sel mdzad pa//legs mnga' de dag spyod pa 'i yi no//gang rnams sems can
73b6(phal chen): kun mkhas shing //chos kyi dbyings la ngo bo nyid kyis zhugs gyur pa'i//bde gshegs gzhan dring mi 'jog pa//namakha' mnyam pa de ltar spyod pa 'di yino//'jig rten kun tu mtha' yas pa//namakhas klas pa yang
73b7(phal chen): dag thugasu chud gyur te//zil gyis mi non chos mnga' ba//thub mchog de dag spyod pa 'di yino//ldog pa med pa de dag rnams//mi dma'i chos dang ldan ba thub par dka' ba dag/smon lam stobs kyi mthar phyin pa//
73b8(phal chen): spyod legs mnga' ba'i spyod yin mnyam bar gyis//mtha' dang dbus ni med pa'i sar//rjesu zhugs shing thaMd gzigs par gyur pa'i rnams//spyod yul zab mo mnga' ba yi//smra ba'i seng+ge'i spyod pa 'di yino//rab tu
74a1(phal chen): @#//dbye ba'i tshig rgyus la//rjesu zhugs shing rgol ba 'dul bar mdzad pa yig/chos rnams rtogs pa mnga' ba'i rnams//thub chen de dag spyod pa 'di yino//'jig rten bde ba skyed mdzad cing //seMn
74a2(phal chen): nyes med thugs dang ldan pa'i rnams//kha lo sgyur ba mnyam med pa'i//yon tan tshad med de dag spyod 'di yin//'jigs pa med pa stobs mdzad cing //sems can thams cad rab tu tshim par mdzad pa'i rnams//
74a3(phal chen): dang zhing rnyog pa med pa yi//dpe med de dag spyod pa 'di yino //dge ba'i thugs dang ldan ba'i rnams//bsam pa rnam par dag cing spros pa med pa yi//dpal gyi gzi brjid spungs rgyas pa//dpa' mchog ldan pa de dag spyod
74a4(phal chen): 'di'o//don gyi pha rol phyin pa'i rnams//yon tan 'byung gnas rnam par dag pa'i thugs mnga' ba//shin tu sems dpa'i blo ldan rnams//dri nas 'phags pa de dag spyod 'di'o//bdag med nyams pa med
74a5(phal chen): pa'i rnams//rnga'i dbyangs rab mnga' ba zhing du gzhol ba dag/chos kyi tshul la gnas pa'i rnams//tshig la nyams med de dag spyod 'di'o//sbyin bas shin tu 'phags pa rnams//bsod nams mtshan rab
74a6(phal chen): brgyas kyang rab tu brgyan pa dag/'gro la phan par mdzad pa'i rnams//blo rab de dag spyod yul 'di yino//ye shes shin tu zab pa'i sar//rjesu zhugs shing blo gros shin tu brtan pa'i rnams//mi g.yo brtan zhing gnas pa
74a7(phal chen): dag/brtul zhugs brtan pa de dag spyod 'di'o/chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa yi//rjesu 'jug pa'i pha rol phyin par gyur pa dag/rnam par 'phrul la brtson pa'i rnams//chos kyi nyi ma de dag spyod 'di'o//mi mnyam
74a8(phal chen): mnyam pa'i blo mnga' rnams//mi gnyis yongasu bsgoms pa'i tinge 'dzin ldan pa//bsam pa shin tu rtogs pa dag/thams cad gzigs pa de dag spyod 'di'o//'jig rten khams la rab 'jug pa//phra dang yangs dang rgya
74b1(phal chen): che'i rnam pa thams cadu//spyod yul thob par gyur pa'i rnams//ye shes ri bo de dag spyod 'di'o//yongasu dag pa'i sems ldan pa//khams rnams gsum po kun tu chags pa med pa dag/tshul khrims brtul zhugs mthar
74b2(phal chen): phyin pa//rnam dag sems ldan de dag spyod 'di'o//nam mkha'i dbyings kyis mthas gtugs pa'i//ye shes 'byam klas mngon par brjod pa la//zhugs te bslabs pa'i blo ldan pa//ye shes stobs can de dag spyod 'di'o//ye
74b3(phal chen): shes gsal ba'i phung po yis//dus gsum chos kyi dbyings rnams bkang ste sgrub byed kyang //sgyid lug yidu mi 'gyur ba//sangs rgyas yul zhugs de dag spyod yulo//kun tu 'gro bar song gyur cing //stobs bcu mnga'
74b4(phal chen): ba'i chos rnams kun kyang shes pa'i rnams//lus kyi chos kyang chags pa med pa//yon tan dpal can de dag spyod 'di'o//sems can mtha' dang dbus med pa//phyogs bcu 'jig rten dag na rgyu bar gyur pa'i
74b5(phal chen): rnams//sems can kun gyi bdag ni skyabs yin pa//'jigs med de dag spyod pa 'di yino//rtag par brtson 'grus gnas 'cha' zhing //nam yang sangs rgyas chos la zhum pa med pa'i rnams//de ni
74b6(phal chen): 'jig rten sbyang phyir te//klu chen de dag spyod pa 'di yin no//dbang po dag gi rgyus shes pa//mos pa'i tshul gyi pha rol phyin par gyur pa'i rnams//sna tshogs khams kyang rtogs gyur pa//mnyam med rtogs gyur de dag spyod
74b7(phal chen): 'di'o//phyogs rnams bcur por chad med pa'i//mtha' dang dbus med rin chen mang pos 'gengs byed kyang //nam yang skyo ba'i yid med pa//dad par byed pa de dag spyod 'di'o//'od zer dra ba mtha' yas pa//kun tu
74b8(phal chen): 'gyed cing 'jig rten snang bar byed pa'i rnams//snang ba'i chos rnams rtogs gyur pa//blo gros bsgoms pa de dag spyod 'di'o/gang zhig phyogs bcu'i zhing gi rnams//khrag khrig bsam gyis mi khyab thaMd
75a1(phal chen): @#//skyod byed kyang //'jigs par 'gyur bar mi byed de//'jig rten phan pa bskyed la rtag tu brtson//tshig gi tshul la mkhas pa'i rnams//brjod pa rnam par dbye ba'i pha rol phyin gyur pa//shes 
75a2(phal chen): pa rmongs med mkhas pa dag/mi sgul sems ldan de dag spyod 'di'o//spyi'u tshugs rgyu la mkhas pa dag/zhing rnams rnam par dbye ba'i pha rolas phyin pa'i rnams//mi zad sa la zhugs pa dag/pham byed blo gros de dag spyod 
75a3(phal chen): 'di'o//yon tan khrag khrig tshad med pa//byang chub rab la gzhol zhing spyad pa spyod pa dag/yon tan thams cad mthar phyin pa//legs pa mi zad de dag spyod 'di'o//'jig rten 'das pa smra ba'i rnams//smra ba'i 
75a4(phal chen): dam pa smra ba'i seng ger gyur pa dag/'gro ba dag bya'i mthar phyin pa//rnam dag sems ldan de dag spyod pa 'di'o//sangs rgyas rnams kyi dbang bskur bas//chos kyi dam pa rab la mngon par dbang bskur zhing //chos kyi 
75a5(phal chen): tshul gyi rjesu song ba'i rnams//yid yangs ldan ba de dag spyod 'di'o//'gro ba'i srid pa kun srung zhing //sems can thams cad rnam par bsgoms pa'i yid ldan ba//shin tu rtogs pa'i mdzod 'chang rnams//
75a6(phal chen): chos kyi ri bo de dag spyod 'di yin//dbyangs kyi yan lag gcig gis kyang //'khor rnams brjod du med pa shes par byed pa dag/dbyangs kyi tshul la zhugs pa'i rnams//chags med mthong ba de dag spyod 'di'o/gang zhig 
75a7(phal chen): tshig lam rjes song zhing //ye shes shin tu 'phags pas rnam par mi rtog pa//rnam par rtog med spyod yul can//rang bzhin med mthong de dag spyod 'di'o//chos kyi rgya mtshor gnas 'cha' zhing //chos rnams 
75a8(phal chen): thams cad rgya yis rab tu 'debs byed pa//mtshan nyid med pa'i tshul ldan rnams//dpal gyi tshul can de dag spyod 'di'o//bde bar gshegs pa'i zhing gcig tu//bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i dus rnams chud byed 
75b1(phal chen): cing //bskal pa grangs med 'dzud byed pa//skyo med brtson ba de dag spyod 'di'o//tha dad mtshan can mi'i dbang//skra'i rtse mo gcig la sangs rgyas brjod yas pa//gang gis de dag mthong gyur rnams//spyod pa bzang ldan
75b2(phal chen): de dag spyod 'di'o//gang gis skra'i cha tsam la//brjod du med ba'i 'dren pa rnams ni mthong gyur cing //de bzhin chos dbyings thams cad de//rgyal sras de dag spyod pa 'di yino//bskal ba brjodu med pa'i rnams//
75b3(phal chen): sems bskyed gcig gis rab tu shes par gyur pa'i rnams//ring dang thung bar nges med pa//rnam thar spyod pa de dag spyod 'di'o//lta zhing don med mi 'gyur la//sangs rgyas spyod pa dag la don yod las ldan rnams/
75b4(phal chen): chags pa med pa'i yid can dag/rgyal ba'i dam pa de dag spyod 'di yin//gang gis sangs rgyas mthong ba'i tshe//bskal pa khrag khrig gnam du 'ang doms par mi 'gyur bar//dga' ba shin tu spyod pa rnams//
75b5(phal chen): blta na don yod de dag spyod 'di'o//sems can khams ni mtha' yas pa//sems shing bskal pa mang po zad par byed pa'i rnams//seMn med par shes pa dag/sems can gtso bo de dag spyod
75b6(phal chen): 'di'o//bsod nams mang po bsags pa'i rnams//ye shes skyob pa bsod nams chen po'i snying po can//bsod nams mu stegs 'gro ba yi//sems can dam pa'i spyod 'di'o//chos dbyings kun tu mtha' yas pa/
75b7(phal chen): nam mkha'i rnam pa bzhin du de dag tshad med pa//tshig gi lam la gnas pa'i rnams//seng+ge'i sgra sgrogs de dag spyod 'di'o//gang zhig tinge 'dzin gcig pa la//tinge 'dzin ni khrag khrig brjodu med pa yi//chos kyi gnas
75b8(phal chen): su zhugs gyur pa//smra ba'i zla ba de dag spyod 'di'o//bzod pa'i stobs kyi mthar phyin pa//de dag chos rnams thams cad zhi la bzod pa yin//mi sgul sems dang mnyam pa'i rnams//mtha' yas shes can de dag
76a1(phal chen): @#//spyod 'di'o//'jig rten khams ni gcig nas kyang //de dag lus ni g.yo zhing 'pho bar mi byed par//'gro ba kun tu 'ang snang ba'i rnams//mtha' yas lus can de dag spyod 'di yin//gang zhig
76a2(phal chen): zhing gi tshul rnams kun//rjesu 'jug pa shin tu phra bar rab shes kyang //yongasu rgyas bya'i blo med pa//mtha' yas sems pa de dag spyod 'di'o//gnas dang gnas min shes gyur cing//stobs la 'jug pa dag tu shin
76a3(phal chen): tu zhugs pa'i rnams//stobs kyi dam pa 'dzin pa dag/stobs kyi stobs chen de dag spyod 'di'o//'das pa dag dang ma 'ongs dang //da ltar byung ba'i las rnams shin tu 'byam klas la//rtag tu shes la 'jug gyur pa//
76a4(phal chen): tshul shes de dag spyod pa 'di yino//'gro ba'i dus dang dus min pa//ji ltar sems can 'dul bar 'gyur de bzhin shes//dus la 'bebs par mkhas pa'i rnams//shes pa de dag spyod pa 'di yino //
76a5(phal chen): chos la spyod pa kun srung zhing //lus dang ngag dang yid kyi rnams kyang srung byed pa//bdud kyi lam du ma zhugs pa//shes pa'i blo can de dag spyod 'di'o//rnam par gzhag pa'i rgyus mkhas pa//
76a6(phal chen): chos rnams la ni ji ltar spobs par 'jug pa'i rnams//de bzhin nyid dang yul mnyam pa//bder gshegs spyod pa de dag spyod 'di yin//chags med mdzod la brtson ba dag/gzungs kyi sgo ma'i dkyil 'khor rab tu
76a7(phal chen): shes pa'i rnams//chos kyi dbyings la 'jug pa yi//rjesu zhugs pa de dag spyod 'di yin //dus gsum gshegs pa'i rgyal ba rnams//ji snyed bzhugs pa kun dang gcig sems yid ldan ba//rang bzhin gcig tu rtogs
76a8(phal chen): pa'i rnams//chags med rigs can de dag spyod 'di'o //bsgribs med ye shes tshul shes shing //bsgribs med ye shes phyag rgya dag la zhugs pa'i rnams//bsgribs med mig kyang bdog byed pa//mig can de dag
76b1(phal chen): spyod pa 'di yino//gnyisu med la spyod pa yi//rdzu 'phrul rnam par gzhog pa'i mgon po thams cad kun//rnam par 'phrul ba mkhyen pa'i rnams//gnyisu med spyod de dag spyod 'di'o//chos kyi sprin ni mtha' yas pa'i//
76b2(phal chen): char chen rab tu 'bebs shing zhing rnams yangs par rgyu//don rnams thams cad rtogs gyur pa//chos sprin de dag spyod yul 'di yino//dad pa phyir ldog mi 'gyur zhing //rtsa ba shin tu tshugs shing dad pa rnam dag pa//
76b3(phal chen): rnam thar shes pa thams cad la//bslabs pa rnams kyi spyod yin mnyan bar gyis//'gro ba 'di dag ji snyed pa//ma lus thams cad sems bskyed gcig tu chud byas te//sems kyi rang bzhin shes pa'i rnams//rang bzhin
76b4(phal chen): med pa de dag spyod 'di yin//zhing rnams bsam gyis mi khyab ba//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun tu gang gis sprul byas shing //mnyam pa med par der spyod pa//dpe med 'gro ldan de dag spyod 'di'o//sangs rgyas zhing
76b5(phal chen): rnams mtha' yas te//de dag kun nas 'jig rten mgon po gang bzhugs pa//de rnams dang ni mngon sum du//sangs rgyas zhabs kyi drung na 'khod gyur pa//de dag lus ni gcig pur yang //tinge
76b6(phal chen): 'dzin la mnyam par gzhag pa rab tu smon//lus ni stong phrag mtha' yas pa//tinge 'dzin las ldang na shin tu snang //spyod pa zab dang ldan byas nas//spros pa med pa'i spyod pa dag la rab spyod pa//dga' ba'i yid dang ldan ba'i rnams//
76b7(phal chen): sems can kun la dga' bar byed pa yin//dbang po tha dad tshul rnams la//blo gros gnas shing shes pa rab tu thob pa'i rnams//dbang po dag ni mi gnas pa//ma dngul 'dul ba 'di dag spyod 'di yin//tha dad chos kyi tshul
76b8(phal chen): rnams la//chos rnams thams cad kun la dbang bas shes gyur pa//kun kyang sangs rgyas mdzad pa la//dmigs pa'i tshogs rab tu spyod pa yin//shin tu yangs pa'i ljags mnga' ba//de dag dbang po rnams dang legs
77a1(phal chen): @#//pa'i spyod thos na//su'i sems shig dad mi 'gyur//namakha' lta bu de dag spyod 'di yin//kun tu mtha' yas shes ldan zhing //chos rnams kun la kun tu mtha' yas mig yod pa//ye shes mtha'
77a2(phal chen): yas tshul ldan ba//mtha' yas shes can de dag spyod 'di yin//zad mi nus pa zad byed cing //bsod nams 'byam klas yod pas bsod nams mi zad pa//'gro ba dag ba'i mthar phyin rnams//'dre med de dag spyod pa 'di yino//
77a3(phal chen): tshogs dang ldan zhing 'jig rten dang //de dag chos kyi laM la rab tu 'jug byas kyang //de dag sems can 'du shes med//sems can bye ba khrag khrig rab tu 'grel//nyon mongs med pa rtogs shes shing //spyod pa mngon bar shes pa
77a4(phal chen): thaMd mthong ba'i rnams//bde ba'i lam rnams ston byed pa//mig can de dag tshul ni 'di yino//sangs rgyas ye shes spyod pa dang //tshad med yon tan dam pa thob par gyur pa'i rnams//mi 'jigs pa ni rnyed gyur pa//
77a5(phal chen): shes can de dag tshul ni 'di yono//'jug pa kun la rab zhugs shing //zhing la 'jug dad chos kyi dbang la zhugs gyur pa//sems can na zhugs la gnas byas nas//sems can dam pa sems can 'grol
77a6(phal chen): bar byed//phyogs rnams kun tu chos kyi rnga//brdungs shing 'jig rten 'di dag ston byed pa'i//chos rab sbyin ba'i bdag po rnams//nyams med rtogs byed de dag sbyong 'di yin//skyil mo krung 'dug gcig gis kyang //zhing 
77a7(phal chen): yangs brjod du med pa kun tu rgyas byed kyang //su'i lus la gnod med pa//chos sku ldan ba de dag spyod 'di yin//don dang yi ge gcig la yang //shin tu bstan pa bye ba khrag khrig rjesu 'breng //de dag mtha' ni rnyed mi nus//
77a8(phal chen): mnyam med shes ldan de dag spyod 'di yin/bsgribs pa med pa'i tshul shes pa//sangs rgyas rnam thar tshul la shin tu bslabs pa'i rnams//'jigs pa med par gyur pa dag/tshad med de dag tshul ni 'di yino//de dag phyogs
77b1(phal chen): rnams rgya mtsho dang //zhing rnams rgya mtsho mtha' ma dag dang dbus med par//shes pa rgya mtsho ldan ba yi//chos rnam rgya mtshos 'jig rten tshim par byed//gang rnams gshegs shing skyer 'gyur dang //byang chub rnam par 'tshang rgya 'gro
77b2(phal chen): ba kun dag tu//su zhig mngon bar snang gyur pa//mtha' dbus med pa de dag spyod 'di yin//mya ngan 'da' ba du ma yang //zhing rnams bye ba khrag khrig dag na mngon bar snang //'on kyang smra ba'i seng ge rnams//mya ngan mi 'da' smon la ma
77b3(phal chen): phyir mi ldog/chos rnams lhun ni gcig gyur cing //sems can kun dang lus kyang gcig tu gyur pa'i rnams//rgyal ba dag la rtog med pas//lus gcig gyur pa spyod 'di yin//tshig dang don rnams tha dad pa//'jig rten khams
77b4(phal chen): rnams mtha' dang dbus yod ma yin bar//rang bzhin gcig la mos pa dag/dus gsum shes pa de dag spyod 'di yin //sems can dag dang rgyal ba dang //chos kyi tshul rnams kun la zad pa med rnyed pa//sangs rgyas byin
77b5(phal chen): la mos pa dag/rgyal bas byin rlabs de dag spyod 'di yin/chags pa med pa'i sku 'dra zhing //chags pa med pa'i mig gi pha rol phyin pa'i rnams//chags med rna ba mkhas pa dag/chags med yid can de dag
77b6(phal chen): spyod 'di yin//mngon shes thams cad rnyed pa dang //ye shes mngon bar shes pa'i rjesu song ba'i rnams//shes pa kun gyi dpal ldan pa//shes pa bzang can de dag spyod 'di yin//shin tu mnyam gzhag sems ldan zhing //
77b7(phal chen): ye shes mtha' yas shes rab shin tu che ba'i rnams//kun tu spyod yul ldan pa dag/thams cad gzigs pa de dag spyod 'di yin/yon tan kun gyi mthar phyin pa//sems can yangs pa gcig nas gcig brgyud sgrol ba'i
77b8(phal chen): rnams//yidani yongasu nyams med pa//rtag tu brtson ba de dag spyod 'di yin/kun mkhyen thams cad gzigs pa dag/sangs rgyas thams cad dus gsum gshegs pa mgon po yi//rigs dang rgyud du skyes pa'i rnams//
78a1(phal chen): @#//rgyal sras de dag spyod pa 'di yino//rjesu bstan la brtson byed pa//phas kyi rgol ba'i lam la 'jigs med spyod pa dag/mtha' yas blo'i spyod pa dang //sangs rgyas byang chub rjesu zhugs pa'i rnams//
78a2(phal chen): mtha' dbus med pa'i zhing gi rnams//thams cad kun du snang ba gcig gis gsal bar byed//thams cad snang bar byed pa'i rnams//mun pa sel ba'i spyod pa 'di yino//gang du rgyal bas mdzad 'os pa//der ni seMn khrag khrig shin tu 'dus byed cing /
78a3(phal chen): sangs rgyas sku dang sangs rgyas zhing //sangs rgyas rgyan can khrag khrig snang bar byed//spyod pa dag ni mtha' yas la//su gnas brtan pa de dag shes par ji ltar rung //spyod pa thams cad shin tu ston//'gro bar spyod pa thams cad mthar thug

103a4(phal chen): pos mngon bar bstodo//lha'i ris gzhan dang gzhan nas 'dus pa'i 'khor de dag thams cad dang /sangs rgyas kyi zhing brjodu med pa nas/'dus pa'i byang chub sems dpa'i 'khor rnams kyang /de bzhin gsheg+sa 
103a5(phal chen): pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas kyi khyu mchog gi/mthu phyung ba bsam gyis mi khyab pa yongasu ston cing /de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku yang dag par 'byung ba dang /bsam gyis mi 
103a6(phal chen): khyab pa'i rdzu 'phrul yang dag par ston pa dang /sems can grangs med pa'i rgyud tshim par mdzad pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi rgyan rnam par phye bas namakha'i khams thams cad dang /chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par mdzad
103a7(phal chen): pa/'gro ba thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba mtha' yas pa brtan bar mdzad pa/sangs rgyas kyi cho 'phrul gzhal du med pa las byung ba/tshig gi lam thams cad las yang dag par 'das pa/byang chub sems dpa' rigs dang 
103a8(phal chen): ldan pa thams cad kyis mngon bar mos par bya ba/sems can 'dul ba ji lta ba bzhin du tshim par bya ba'i phyir/sangs rgyas kyi sku yangs pa rjesu khyab par gyur pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba brjod pa thams cad rnam par dag pas/
103b1(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi yon tan tshad med par yang dag par ston pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i yul rgyun mi 'chad par 'byung ba'i sangs rgyas kyi tinge 'dzin las byung ba/'gro ba thams cad ma lus pa'i lus kyi rjesu bdal ba/sku'i byin
103b2(phal chen): gyi rlabs la sa mtshams med pa/yid tshad med pa yongasu mgu bar mdzad pa/thams cad mkhyen pa nyid kyi rigs rgyun mi gcod pa/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa mtha' dang dbus med pa 'byung ba brnyes pa/'das pa dang /ma byon
103b3(phal chen): pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rigasu byung ba/khams dang /mos pa bgrang du med pa thams cad sbyong bar mdzad pa/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad ye shes mngon par bsgrub pa la 'jug par mdzad pa/byang
103b4(phal chen): chub sems dpa' thams cadakyi dbang po rab tu dga' bar mdzad pa/chos kyi sprin gyis namakha'i khams thams cadu yog par mdzad pa/chos kyi dbyings thams cad ma lus par de bzhin gshegs pas 'dul
103b5(phal chen): ba'i byin gyis rlabs kyis rlob pa/sems can thams cad kyi rtog pa/bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du yongasu rgyas par mdzad pa/mi gnas pa'i ye shes kyi rgyud kyis 'gro ba thams cad las 'phags pa/ye
103b6(phal chen): shes kyi rnam pa thams cad brnyes pa/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs snang ba gzigs pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba ji ltar bskyed pa bzhin du yang dag par gzigs pa/sems bskyed pa rgya chen po skye bar mdzad pa/'gro ba thams cad kun
103b7(phal chen): tu bzang po thug pa med pa'i ye shes yang dag par rgyu bas byung ba/'gro ba dang zhing thams cadu khyab pa/chos kyi dbyings thamasacad du mi ldog par gshegs pa/sangs rgyas kyi sku'i rgyud brjodu med pa'i yang brjod du med pa yang
103b8(phal chen): dag par ston pa/bsam pa ji lta ba bzhIn du 'khrul pa mi mnga' bar byin gyis rlob pa/'gro ba thaMd kyi tshig gi rnam pas yon tan mi zad pa/sangs rgyas rjesu dran pas/chos kyi dbyings thams cad rgyas par mdzad pa/'jig
104a1(phal chen): @#//rten gyi khams mtha' dang dbus med par khyab bar mdzad pa/byang chub kyi sems kyis/ji ltar dam bcas pa'i sems can rim gror mdzad pa/ji ltar brnyes pa'i chos kyi sbyin ba mdzad pa/seMn
104a2(phal chen): ji ltar 'dul ba bzhin du 'dul bar mdzad pa/mos pa dang bsam ba ji lta ba bzhin du rnam par dag par mdzad pa/gzugs kyi sku bsam gyis mi khyab par byin gyis rlob pa/'gro ba thams cad la gnas pa mi mnga' zhing mnyam
104a3(phal chen): ba nyid kyi rjesu bzhud pa/spyod pa tha dad pa las byung ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs bcu bsgribs pa med pa/mngon par sangs rgyas pa/thugs la chags pa mi mnga' bas rtag tu mnyam bar bzhag cing ma 'dres pa/yais
104a4(phal chen): thams cad la rnam par spyod pa/don dang tshig dang yi ge tha dad pa yang dag para ston pa/ye shes rgya mtsho mtha' dang dbus med pa la zhugs pa/yon tan gyi mchog ye shes kyi snying pos byung ba/chos kyi dbyings 
104a5(phal chen): thams cad sangs rgyas kyi nyi ma shar ba/'gro ba thams cadu sngon gyi smon lam zad ba mi mnga' bar chos kyi dbyings kyi rang bzhin la gnas pa/sangs rgyas kyi rnam par spyod pa ma nyams pa brnyes pa/
104a6(phal chen): bdag gi dang yongasu gzung pa med pas gnas pa mi mnga' ba/'jig rten las 'das pa'i chos thaMd 'byung zhing /'jig rten gyi chos thams cad kyis ma gos pa 'gro ba thaMd kyi mi shes pa'i rgyal mtshan yang dag par
104a7(phal chen): bcad pa/'jig rten thaMd kyi mi shes pa chen po yongasu bsal ba'i gnasu gyur pa/'gro ba thams cad snang bar mngon bar 'don pa/'jig rten thaMd ye shes khyab par du 'phags pa la rab tu gzhog pa/'gro ba thaMd
104a8(phal chen): nye bar 'tsho ba'i bsod nams yongasu mi zad pa/byang chub sems dpa' thams cad kyi shes pa thugasu chud pas dad par bya ba 'os pa/yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyasu gzhol ba'i byams pa chen po las byung ba
104b1(phal chen): thugs rje chen po yang dag par ston pas spyod pa/sangs rgyas kyi sku brjod du med pa'i chos dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos sna tshogs bsam gyis mi khyab pa'i gsung dang dbyangs kyi char 'phebs pa/sems can gyi bsam pa ji lta 
104b2(phal chen): ba bzhin du yang dag par tshim par mdzad pa/sngon gyi mtha' dang /phyi ma'i mtha' dang /da ltar byung ba'i mtha' dang ni gas pa'i byin rlabs shin tu dag cing bsam gyis mi khyab pas sems can dag par mdzad pa/byang chub
104b3(phal chen): sems dpa' thams cad lung bstan pa rgyun mi 'chad pa/sangs rgyas kyi rigs su dbang bskur ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i rgyud du byung ba/'jig rten thams cad 'khor lo mi ldog par 'khor ba/'jig rten thams can
104b4(phal chen): kyi mngon bar zhen pa thams cad las 'phags pa/sangs rgyas kyi zhing dang /chos kyi dbyings thams cad kyi mtha' klas pa la mi gnas pa/'gro ba thams cad mnyam pa nyid du thugs su chud pa/'jig rten las
104b5(phal chen): 'das pa'i bsod nams kyis rnam par dag pa/'khor ba la mi bzhugs par 'jig rten thams cad snang bar gyur pa/btsal ba mi dmigs pas/chos kyi dbyings thams chad kyi rang bzhin ye
104b6(phal chen): shes kyi zla ba/de bzhin gshegs pa tshad med pa'i rjes su bzhud pa/sgyu ma lta bu'i ye shes kyis byin gyis rlob pa/mig yor lta bu'i ye shes kyis rnam par ston pa/rmi lam lta bur rjes su dran pa/sprul pa dang mnyam
104b7(phal chen): bar byung ba/sems kyi rang bzhin lta bur 'jig rten sna tshogs thams cad byin gyis rlob pa/las kyi rnam par smin pa'i tshul dang /bsam pa ji lta ba bzhin du/sems can gyi skye mched rnam par phye ba yang
104b8(phal chen): dag par ston pa/sems can gyi dbang po dang 'thun par sangs rgyas kyi sku byung ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i dmigs pa la sems can grangs med pa'i sems yang dag par gnas pa/'jig rten thams cad rten cing
105a1(phal chen): @#//'brel bar 'byung ba rnam par bstan pa'i rang bzhin du/chos kyi mtshan nyid kyis yang dag par spyod pa/ye shes kyi rgyud mtshan nyid med pa'i rang bzhin dang /mtshan nyid gcig tu gyur pa/
105a2(phal chen): 'jig rten thams cad kyi rang bzhin gyi mtshan nyid snang ba mngon bar bsgrub pa'i spyod pa yang dag par ston cing /'gro ba thams cad gdon bar brtson ba/sangs rgyas kyi spyod yul bla na med pa yang dag par ston pa/'jig
105a3(phal chen): rten dang zhing thams cad yongasu skyob pas/sangs rgyas kyi mtshan ma gzung bar 'jig rten du mngon bar byung ba/'jig rten gyi nyon mongs pa'i mtshan ma thams cad rnam par bstsal ba/'jig rten thams cad kyi rjesu dran pa 
105a4(phal chen): dang /dmigs par 'byor par gyur pa/'khrug pa med pa/khong du chud par 'gyur ba'i rgyu theg pa chen pos zhal yongasu rgyas pa/chos thams cad kyi don grub pa/'gro ba thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa bas rnam par 
105a5(phal chen): phye ba nyams pa med pa'i 'phrin las rnam par smin pas rnam par dag pa ye shes kyi 'byung gnas dus gsum mnyam ba nyidu thugasu chud pa/'jig rten thams cad yongasu rtog pa/yongasu mi rtog pas 
105a6(phal chen): byung ba/'jig rten gyi khams thams cad 'od zer gyi dra bas bsal bar gyur pa/phyogs bcu 'byam klas pa thams cad sangs rgyas kyi 'od gyis snang bar mdzad pa/sangs rgyas thams cad kyi rjesu bzhud pa blta 
105a7(phal chen): bas mi ngoms shing btang mi bra ba/yon tan chen po dang /ye shes dang /mngon bar shes pa dang /byang chub sems dpa'i spyod pa 'byung ba/dbang po mngon bar mkhyen pa'i ye shes kyi spyod yul can/ye shes kyi 
105a8(phal chen): dkyil 'khor gyis sangs rgyas kyi mdzad pa rgyas par mdzad pa/sangs rgyas kyi 'phrin las shin tu sgrub pa nub cing 'das pa dang /ma 'ongs pa dang /da ltar byung ba'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i tshul ston pa/byang chub 
105b1(phal chen): sems dpa'i gzungs mtha' yas pa'i char 'bebs pa/bsam ba yangs pa'i bsgom ba yongasu 'dzin par mdzad pa/sangs rgyas kyi dpal thams cad kyis rab tu 'bar ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku kha dog mtha' yas
105b2(phal chen): pa sna tshogasu 'jig rten thams cad la mngon bar ston pa/sgrib pa'i chos thams cad du ma lhung ba/chos thams cad kyi rang bzhin gyis don gyis dag pa/yon tan gyi chos thams cad la mnga' bas/mdzes pa'i dkyil 
105b3(phal chen): 'khor can/yon tan gyi 'od zer mnga' ba'i nyi mar gyur pa/bsod nams kyi zhing du shar ba/gzi brjid thams cad kyi dra bas mngon bar skyes pa/'jig rten thams cad du sangs rgyas kyis sprul pa yang dag par
105b4(phal chen): 'dzin pa/'jig rten thams can sprul par thugasu chud pa'i ye shes snang ba dang ldan ba/de bzhin gshegs pa'igo 'phang chags pa med pa mnga' ba phyi ma'i mtha'i yon tan 'jig rten thams cad kyi rnam par shes pa/
105b5(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad dang 'thun bar ston pa'i ye shes kyi phyag mnga' ba/de bzhin gshegs ba ye shes kyi sman pa/sems can thaMd bde bar mdzad pa/'jig rten tshad med pa thams cad shin 
105b6(phal chen): tu rnam par dag pas rjesu 'brang ba/'gro ba dang zhing 'byam klas pa thams cad phyir mi ldog pa/ye shes kyi spyan phubs rab rib med cing rnam par dag pa/sangs rgyas kyi lo legs pa yang dag par ston pa/'khu
105b7(phal chen): ba'i sems can gyi khams lasu rung bar mdzad pa/'dul ba sna tshogs du ma la 'jug pa/dus bzhin du 'dul ba'i rgyun mi 'chad pa/'gro ba thams cad la mnyam pa'i thugs kyis yang dag par spyod pa las/skyon med
105b8(phal chen): pa'i rnam par smin pa dang /mya ngan las 'das pa yang dag par ston ba bsam ba dang /las dang rgyu 'thun ba'i 'bras bu ji lta ba bzhin du sangs rgyas kyi sku rnam par ston pa/sangs rgyas kyi sku ji lta ba bzhin du sems can
106a1(phal chen): @#//gyis chos shes pa 'byung bar mdzad pa/sangs rgyas kyis sprul pa byin gyis rlob pa/'jig rten gyi chos thams cad la rnam par gzigs pa'i ye shes mnga' ba/dga' ba chen po dang
106a2(phal chen): lhag pa'i bsam pas dbang po dga' bar mdzad pa/sangs rgyasamathong ba'i dad pa'i shugs mtha' yas pas byung ba/sems can thaMd kyi rang gi dge ba'i rtsa ba phyir mi ldog par mdzad pa/sems can gyi las ji lta ba
106a3(phal chen): bzhin du rjesu 'brang ba/'gro ba gnyis kyis log pa thams cad sad par mdzad pa/sangs rgyas skye bas dge ba yang dag par 'phel ba mdzad pa/sems can la sangs rgyas kyi bag dbab pa/chen po stobs pa mdzad
106a4(phal chen): pa/'jig rten gyi lta ba thams cad la mi gnas pa/ye shes kyi don la rab tu gzhog pa/rab tu gzhag pa'i ye shes kyi tshul gyi spyod yul thugasu chud pa/sangs rgyas kyis gzigs pa'i zil gyis ma
106a5(phal chen): non pa'i rgyan gyis brgyan pa/chos kyi rgyud thugasu chud pa'i ri bo rnam par dag pa/sangs rgyas 'byam klas pa dang /byang chub sems dpa' gzhan dang gzhan du ston/kha na ma tho ba medapa'i sa la seMn
106a6(phal chen): rab tu 'god pa/sangs rgyas kyi yon tan grangs med pas rnam par bris pa/las kyis sna tshogasu gyur pa'i 'jig rten du/de bzhin gshegs pa sna tshogs yang dag par ston pa/sangs rgyas kyi 'byung gnas rnam par
106a7(phal chen): sbyong ba/ye shes thams cad yongasu 'thob par mdzad pa/'jig rten thams cad kyi smon lam rjesu srung ba/mi shigs pa/'gro ba thams cad la dge ba'i bshes gnyen ston pa/snang ba dri ma med cing mchog tu dag par
106a8(phal chen): mdzad pa/'gro ba ma lus pa thams cad kyis ji tsam du mngon sum du mthong ba de tsam du brjodu med pa/sems can thams cad kyi 'gro ba'i rjesu song ba'i byin gyi rlabs mnga' ba/grogs sngon du 'gro ba'i dge ba'i
106b1(phal chen): rtsabs/sems can rnam par dag par mdzad pa/'gro ba thams cad kyi smon lam gyi rjesu song ba/nyams pa med pa/sems can thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba yongasu bzung bas dag par mdzad pa las bstsags pa'i tshul 
106b2(phal chen): gyis yongasu byung ba/bdud kyi dkrugs ma thams cad rnam par 'jom par byung ba/chags pa med pa'i mtha'i stobs kyis byung ba/'gro ba thams cad kyis blta na 'od shin tu dang bar snang ba ye shes kyi dbyings chags pa 
106b3(phal chen): med pa'i snying po'i nyi ma sems can gang yang rung ba la chos kyi sbyin ba mdzad pa/'od zer gyi dkyil 'khor gyi mdzod mtha' dang dbus med pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i stobs kyi ye shes kyi dkyil 'khor mnga' ba/
106b4(phal chen): 'gro ba thams cad la snang bar gyur pa/bsam pa dang yang dag par rtogs pa thams cad yongasu rdzogs pa/phyir rgol ba shin tu bcom ba/yon tan gyi gnas dam pa mchog tu gyur pa/'gro ba thams cad kyi rten 
106b5(phal chen): du gyur pa/yon tan tshad med par 'gyur ba/'gro ba thams cad yongasu dag par mdzad pa/dge ba'i rtsa ba chung ngu tshad med par yongasu 'dzin pa/zad mi shes pa'i ye shes kyi sar 'dzud cing rab 
106b6(phal chen): tu gzhog pa/'jig rten thams cad kyi dge ba'i rtsa ba sems la rag lus par yid ches pas/tshad med pa'i bsod nams dang yon dag par mdzad pa/ye shes kyi tshul gyis ngan song gsum gyi 'gro ba yongasu skyob   
106b7(phal chen): pa'o zhes rnam pa de lta bur/de bzhin gshegs pa la mos pa rnam pa de lta bur yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas la yang dag par lta ba/rnam pa de lta bur ye shes kyi mu stegs las 'bab pa rnam pa de lta 
106b8(phal chen): bu'i yon tan rgya mtsho'i gangs chen mtsho thams cad la 'jug pa/rnam pa de lta bu'i ye shes kyi namakha'i rjesu phyin par rjesu 'gro ba/rnam pa de lta bur 'gro ba thams cad kyi bsod nams kyi zhing du yang dag pa ji lta

107a3(phal chen): cad yang dag par bstan par rjesu sems pa de dag gis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku/bcom ldan 'das kyi ba spu'i sgo re re nas 'od zer bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong grangs med pa 'byung ba grangs med par rnam par dag pa/

107a6(phal chen): gyur to//'od kyi 'khor lo bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong bsam gyis mi khyab pa yang de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku las rnam par 'phrul pa sna tshogs kyis 'phrul zhing 'byung ba/kha dog bsam gyis mi khyab pa/mdog
107a7(phal chen): bsam gyis mi khyab pa/'od bsam gyis mi khyab pa/chos kyi dbyings mtha' dang dbus med pa/thams cad rgyas par byed pa/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyis rlabs dang /sems can rnam par dag pas/dri zhim por rab tu

107b2(phal chen): skye ba brjodu med pa yang dag par ston pa/ye shes kyi sgo la rab tu gzhog pa/don yangs pa shin tu ston pa/sangs rgyas kyi sprul pa brjodu med pa'i tshul yang dag par ston pa/sangs rgyas kyi yul mtha' dang dbus

107b4(phal chen): thams cad mkhyen pa chags pa med pa/mtha' dang dbus med pa'i sangs rgyas kyi spyod pa las byung ba/de bzhin gshegs pa bsam gyis mi khyab pas brten pa'i dpal yod pa/rin po che'i 'od 'phro ba
107b5(phal chen): bye ba brgya stong bsam gyis mi khyab pa ltar byung ba mthong bar gyur to//bcom ldan 'das kyis kyang byin gyis rlabs kyis rgyan smon lam chen po las byung ba'i dge ba'i rtsa ba mtha' yas pa
107b6(phal chen): dang /de bzhin gshegs pa mnyes par bya ba'i spyod pas rnam par dag pa dang /sngon gyi bag yod pa las yang dag par byung ba'i thams cad mkhyen pa'i sems chags pa med pa'i dge ba'i rtsa ba las byung ba dang /
107b7(phal chen): de bzhin gshegs pa 'byam klas pa/'gro ba thams cad kyi the tsom gcod pa dang /de bzhin gshegs par mthong ba la sems can tshad med pa rab tu dgod pa dang /dge ba'i rtsa ba'i sgo dang /sangs rgyas kyi cho

108a1(phal chen): @#//kyi byin gyis rlabs pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi chos bsam gyis mi khyab pa yongasu brjod pa'i tshul gyis/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 'khor du ji ltar sangs rgyas kyi zhing 'byam klas pa khrag 
108a2(phal chen): khrig brgya stong brjod med pa grangs med pa dag na/sems ma dad pa rnams dad par bya ba dang /sems dad pa'i rnams rnam par 'phel bar bya ba dang /sems rnam par 'phel ba'i rnams rnam par dag par bya ba dang /
108a3(phal chen): sems rnam par dag pa'i rnams yongasu smin par bya ba dang /sems yongasu smin pa'i rnams 'dul bar bya ba dang /chos zab mo khong du chud par bya ba dang /ye shes snang ba mtha' dang dbus med par khong du chud 
108a4(phal chen): par bya ba dang /sems tshad med pa'i rnams rgya che bar bya ba dang /thams cad mkhyen pa nyid du sems bskyed pa las phyir mi ldog par bya ba dang /chos nyid dang mi 'gal bar bya ba dang /yang dag pa'i mthas mi

113b5(phal chen): lus dang /de bzhin gshegs bar gyur pa'i spyod yul thaMd du 'gro ba dang /ye shes chen po'i rgyud kyi sgo rnam par phye ba dang /ye shes kyi stobs rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan gyi mthu'i mchog thob pa 
113b6(phal chen): dang /rdo rje gcod pa'i tinge 'dzin gyi tshul rab tu thob pa dang /sems can bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong grangs med pa'i rnams yongasu smin par gdul ba la rmongs pa med pa dang /sangs rgyas kyi rnam par 'phrul pa thaMd 
113b7(phal chen): du 'gro ba/gdon mi za ba'i mchog thob pa dang /bgrang ba thams cad la mngon par ma chags pa dang /bgrang ba mngon bar bsgrub pa thams cad shin tu bslabs pa dang /bgrang ba'i shes pa thams cad kyis 
113b8(phal chen): mthar thug cing /chos rnam par dgod pa la mkhas pa/'di dag dang gzhan yang /yon tan bye ba khrag khrig brgya stod brjod du med pa/yon tan bsngags pa rnam par dag pa mi zad pas/byang chub sems dpa' rnams 
114a1(phal chen): @#//kyi dus gsum gyi yon tan tshad med pa dang ldan ba sha stag/de bzhin gshegs pa mngon sum du bya ba dang /sangs rgyas 'byam klas pa thams cad blta ba'i phyir kun nas 'dus par
114a2(phal chen): gyur to//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//yang dag sangs rgyas skye ba med//rgyal 
114a3(phal chen): ba mya ngan mi 'da' ste//rgyu 'thun pa'i smon lam gyis//des ni rnam par 'phrul ba snang//'di dag chos ni bsam mi khyab//'di dag sems kyi spyod yul min//gang gis ye shes pha rol phyin//des ni sangs
114a4(phal chen): rgyas spyod yul mthong//lus 'di yang dag sangs rgyas min//sgra 'ang de bzhin gshegs pa min//lus dang sgra las gzhan na yang //sangs rgyas rnam par 'phrul mi mthong //sngon gyi las rnams
114a5(phal chen): ma gtogs par//rang byung rnams kyi spyod yul gyi//mchog gi tshula rnams shes pa ni//blo gzhan rnams kyis mi nuso//yang dag sangs rgyas rnam 'phrul 'di//gang dag nas kyang ma 'ongs te//
114a6(phal chen): du 'ang 'gro bar mi 'gyuro//'di dag rang byung sku yin no//tshad med shin tu 'dren pa rnams//chos 'di rab tu bshad mdzad kyang //chos la gnas pa gang yang med//zhing rnams bye ba kun na snang//mkhyen pas
114a7(phal chen): mtha' dang dpung med de//chos kun thugasu chud cing rtogs//chos kyi dbyings la rab zhugs nas//rnam par 'phrul ba ston pa mdzad//chos rnams kun la chags pa med//'gro la de ni bsgribs mi mnga'//
114a8(phal chen): tshad med pa yi yon tan gyis//zhing rnams kun na rab tu snang //gang zhig ye shes 'di dag dang //ye shes thams cad rtogs 'dod pas//bsam pa rnam par sbyang byas te//byang chub spyod pa spyad par bya//
114b1(phal chen): drang srong chen po'i byin gyis rlabs//'di 'dra gang gis mthong gyur cing //de ni the tsom mi byed na//dge bshes mchog la brten pa yin//de nas byang chub sems dpa' dam pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi
114b2(phal chen): byin gyis rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas nas//de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//shin tu zab ste brjod du med//brjod du yod kyang 'da' bar dka'//tshig gi lam las shin tu 'das//nam mkha' la
114b3(phal chen): ni rgyal ba snang //de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin rlabs kyis//mi'i seng ge mthong bar 'gyur//yang dag sangs rgyas rtog med kyang //rgyal ba rnam par rtog par snang //'jig rten mgon pos chos nyid
114b4(phal chen): dngos//zab mo 'di dag yang dag bstan//gang zag mtshungs pa med pa yi//des ni sku 'dir rab tu 'byung //thams cad gzigs kyi spyod yul gyi//ye shes chen po 'di 'dra ste//'di la gang zhig
114b5(phal chen): rtogs 'dod pa//des na rgyal ba mngon du byos//yid kyi dbang po rnam dag pas//de bzhin gshegs pa bsnyen bkur zhing //'di'i tshul las 'da' byed tshe//nam yang skyo bar mi bya'o//
114b6(phal chen): mi zad pa'i bsod nams dang //byang chub sems kyis brtan pa'i rnams//the tsom rnam par bzlog byas te//de dag la ni skyo med ltos//rgyal sras gang gis chos dbyings kyi//shin tu bstan pI
114b7(phal chen): mthar phyin pa//des ni kun gzigs rnam 'phrul pa//'di dag shin tu 'da' bar bya//dad pa chos 'di'i rtsa ba zhes//chos kyi phung pos bshad mdzad kyis//dad pa shin tu cher skyed la//
114b8(phal chen): byang chub spyod pa spyad par gyis//sangs rgyas sti stang gang byed cing //de bzhin gshegs la dril gzo ba//yang dag sangs rgyas dpal ldan las//de dag rgyang mar mi gnaso//bdag nyid che ba 'di lta bu//gang zhig thos mthong
115a1(phal chen): @#//mkhas pa rnams//spyod yul thob par cir 'gyur ba'i//smon lam su zhig stong mi byed//de nas byang chub sems dpa' dpa' ba'i rgyal mtshan gyis/sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam
115a2(phal chen): par bltas nas/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//ji ltar nyi ma'i 'od snang bas//mig gis gzugs rnams mthong gyur pa//de bzhin sangs rgyas byin rlabs kyis//dag pa'i sems ldan rgyal ba mthong //ji ltar brtson 'grus
115a3(phal chen): stobs ldan pa//rgya mtsho la ni 'jug par byed//de bzhin ye shes stobs ldan bas//bsam du med pa'i sangs rgyas mthong //ji ltar sa bon rnam pa dag/zhing bzang la ni skyer 'gyur ba//de bzhin sems bsam rnam dag la//sangs
115a4(phal chen): rgyas chos ni skye bar 'gyur//ji ltar nor gyi phung po yis//dbul ba thaMd spong byed pa//de bzhin sangs rgyasachos rnams kyis//byang chub sems dpa' rnam dag 'gyur//ji ltar dug tu gyur pa la//nad med dug
115a5(phal chen): snan rgyal bar byed//de bzhin nyon mongs rnam pa la//rnam dag tshul ni spyod yulo//sangs rgyas kyis ni gang bsngags pa'i//dge ba'i bshes la brten byas na//de yi mthus ni sangs rgyas kyi//chos nyid
115a6(phal chen): 'di dag thos par 'gyur//bskal pa bye ba grangs med par//sbyin pa mchog rnams sbyin byas kyang //'di'i tshul la rab spyod pa'i//grangs dang char yang mi 'gro'o//sangs rgyas yon tan mtha' yas te//mtshan gyis shin tu
115a7(phal chen): rnam par bris//brjod pas yang dag sangs rgyas rnams//rgyal ba shes par mi nuso//rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rkang gnyis mchog/gnas nas g.yo ba mi mnga' bar//sku ni kun tu rnam rgyu zhing //chos kyi dbyings ni ston pa mdzad//
115a8(phal chen): ji ltar nam mkha'i khams 'di ni//nam yang rang bzhin mi 'gyur te//de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyid kyang //ngo bo nyid las 'gyur ba med//de nas byang chub sems dpa' skar ma'i rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs
115b1(phal chen): kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//mi'i 'jig rten 'di kun dang //lha'i gnas rnams thaMd dang//'gro ba ma lus kun na yang //mi'i gtso bo shin tu snang //ji ltar sems dngos gcig
115b2(phal chen): pu las//rnam pa tha dad sems brjod pa//de bzhin sangs rgyas sku gcig las//sangs rgyas thams cad brjod pa yin//byang chub gnyisu med par brjod//byang chub de yang mtshan nyid med//mtshan gyis mngon bar rnam bris pa'i//
115b3(phal chen): sku rnams gnyisu snang bar gyur//mkha' dang chos kyi dbyings la zhugs//sgyu ma'i sa yang rab tu bstan//de phyir sangs rgyas de dag gi/spyod yul blta ba zad pa med//yang dag sangs rgyas chos kyi sku//mgon po thams cad
115b4(phal chen): dus gsum du//ji ltar 'dul ba'i 'gro ba la//gzugs kyi skur yang rab tu snang //'di 'dra'i lus su bdag gyur ces//sangs rgyas rtog par mi mdzad//ston la rnam rtog mi mnga' yang //byin gyi rlabs ni
115b5(phal chen): ston par mdzad//dbyer med pa'i dbyings la yang //chos kyi dbyings ni yang dag gnas//'jig rten khams ni tshad med par//thub pa pas rnam par rtog pa bstan//sku ni sprul pa ma yin te//sprul min ba
115b6(phal chen): 'ang sku ma yin//sprul pa med pa'i chos 'di yis//sprul pa rnams ni rab tu bstan//yang dag sangs rgyas tshad med pa//chos kyi dbyings la shin tu rten//mtha' dang dbung med rab tu zab//tshig gi lam gyis bzla 'bar
115b7(phal chen): dka'//kun tu 'gro ba'i chos rnams la//de bzhin gshegs pa don nges mkhyen//zhing rnams kun la chags mi mnga'//chos kyi dbyings la rnam par spyod//de nas byang chub sems dpa' ye shes rgyal mtshan gyis//
115b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//kun mkhyen ye shes ma chags pa//de la zhugs par gang gyur pa//de'i sems ni tshad mi zin//byang chub kyi spyad pa
116a1(phal chen): @#//spyod//de bzhin gshegs pa mang po rnams//yul dang yul du de snang //chos rnams phyogs gcig gang la yang //yang dag sangs rgyas gnas pa med//'jig rten sgro ma de dag gis//byin
116a2(phal chen): gyi rlabs ni gang bstan pa//bskal pa bsam gyis mi khyab par//'di'i mtha' ni rnyed par dka'//dus gsum rnams ni ji snyed pa'i//sems can rnams kyi mthar thug pa//bgrad ba'i mthar ni phyin par nus//
116a3(phal chen): 'dren pa la ni mthar thug med//de dag sku ni gcig tu snang //sku gnyis de dag bsam mi khyab//'jig rten khamasu snang gyur kyang //sku la gnyisu gyur pa med//ji ltar zla ba shar gyur pa//tshad med pa'i gzugs
116a4(phal chen): brnyan rnams//chu'i snodu snang gyur kyang //zla ba gnyisu gyur pa med//de bzhin chags med mkhyen ldan rnams//chags med rnam par 'tshang rgya ba//zhing rnams kun na rab tu snang //sangs rgyas la ni gnyis kyang
116a5(phal chen): med//'di la gcig kyang yong ma yin//sku ni gnyis sam gsum du med//ji ltar sems can bsam pa bzhin//tshad med par ni rab tu snang //sku ni 'das pa ma yin te//ma 'ongs pa'ang sku ma yin//
116a6(phal chen): skyes dang byang chub mya ngan 'das//skad gcig gis ni rab tu snang //ji ltar sgyu mar gyur pa'i gzugs//skyes pa ma yin 'ongs pa min//de lta de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku//skye ba med par shin tu bstan//de nas byang chub
116a7(phal chen): sems dpa' rin po che'i rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//yang dag sangs rgyas tshad med par//chadu sku yang rnam par bstan//
116a8(phal chen): ji ltar 'dul ba'i seMn rnams//de bzhin 'dren pa rab tu mthong //sku la phyogs ni med par brjod//sangs rgyas yul ni ma lus khyab//bar snang nam mkha' ji lta ba//de bzhin sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//der ni sems
116b1(phal chen): kyang 'pho ba med//sems la skye ba yod pa'ang min//sangs rgyas rnams kyi spyod yul ni//skye ba med la rab tu gnas//ji ltar rab rib ldan ba'i mig/nang na med cing phyi na'ang med//de bzhin 'jig rten sangs rgyas dag/nang na
116b2(phal chen): mi bzhugs phyi na 'ang min//'jig rten kun don gyi phyir//rkang gnyis kyi ni mchog byung ste//'jig rten du ni ma byung yang //'jig rten dag tu skye bos mthong //sangs rgyas rnams la zhing mi gnas//rgyal ba dag la nyin
116b3(phal chen): mtshan med//skad cig ma yin zla ba min//rgyal ba la ni lo mi gnas//'jig rten dag na ji skad brjod//nyi ma che ge mo zhig tu//rang byung byang chub rgyal bas thob//sangs rgyas nyi ma'i rjes mi 'brang //de la bskal ba
116b4(phal chen): yod ma yin//dus kyang ma lus med pa ste//dus gsum yang dag rdzogs sangs rgyas//mgon po rnams ni mnga' bar 'gyur//ji ltar nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor ni//nams kyang mtshan dang mi gnas te//nyin mtshan
116b5(phal chen): zhes kyang rab tu brjod//de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyi do//bskal pa gcig dang dus gsum du//'dren pa bzhugs pa ma yin te//dus gsum zhes kyang rab tu brjod/sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos nyid
116b6(phal chen): bltos//de nas byang chub sems dpa' rdul dang bral ba'i rgyal mtshan gyis sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//yang dag sangs rgyas sku gcig ste/
116b7(phal chen): 'dren pa thams cad don gcig kyang //tshad med gzhan dang gzhan dag tu//phyogs bcu kun na rab tu snang //gang gis ma lus thams cad kyi//chos kyi dbyings rnams kun khyab pa//seMn gtso bor thub pa yis//spyod
116b8(phal chen): yul rnams ni ci 'dra ltos//sangs rgyas rnams kyi chos nyi ni//nang gi skus kyang snang ba yin//de bzhin gshegs pa'i byin gyis rlabs//phyi rol skus kyang mi snango//dus mang kun du las rnams kyis//ji ltar las
117a1(phal chen): @#//rnams bstsags pa bzhin//de bzhin saur phye ba'i skus//rigasu byung bar rab tu bstan//rgyal ba'i chos nyid ma gtogs par//sangs rgyas sku ni 'di 'dra ba//grangasu bgrang bar mi nus te//bsam
117a2(phal chen): bas lta yang shes mi nus//ji ltar bdag cag sems kyi las//bsam gyis mi khyab mi 'byung ba//de bzhin sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//sems kyi 'phrin las mi mnga'o//ji ltar zhing rnams bsam yas pa//
117a3(phal chen): sa la rnam par dag pas mdzes//de bzhin sangs rgyas ba sam mi khyab//'gro ba dag na mdzes par snang //ji ltar las rnams 'jug pa ni//rkyen gyis rab tu 'byung bar 'gyur ba//de bzhin rgyu rnams 'thun ba las//
117a4(phal chen): sangs rgyas shin tu mthong bar 'gyur//yid bzhin nor bu'i rgyal po ni//ji ltar yid bsam rdzogs byed pa//de bzhin sangs rgyas chos nyid kyang //rtag tu smon lam rdzogs par sgrub//mtha' dang dbung med 
117a5(phal chen): 'jig rten 'dir//'jig rten mgon po 'dren par gyur//de'i smon lam de dag gis//phyogs bcu 'di dag rgyal bas gang //de nas byang chub sems dpa' dri ma med pa'i rgyal mtshan gyis//
117a6(phal chen): sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te//de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//ye shes dkyil 'khor sangs rgyas te//'jig rten chos rnams rnam par sbyong //'jig rten chos rnams
117a7(phal chen): rnam sbyangs nas//sangs rgyas chos rnams rab tu brjod//de ste sems can mnyam sangs rgyas//sems can la las bsam byas na//rgyal ba de dag thams cad mthong //sangs rgyas la ni gshegs pa med//
117a8(phal chen): sangs rgyas la ni dmigs byas nas//sems kyis sangs rgyas dmigs pas na//sems bskyed pa dang rgyal mnyam par//de bzhin de dag rgyal ba mthong //
117b1(phal chen): dkar ba'i chos kyis nyer brtan cing //gang gis grags pa rnam sbyangs pa//de dag thams cad sems bskyed la//shes rab ldan ba rab tu bzhugs//dmigs pa dag dang byin gyi rlabs//mi'i gtso bo ston pa mdzod//de phyir
117b2(phal chen): dmigs pa kun tu yang //sangs rgyas rgyal ba gshegs par brjod//'jig rten khams rnams 'di dag ni//sangs rgyas sku rnams bdag gi med//sems la bdag gi med pa bstan//byang chub rtogs la bdag gi
117b3(phal chen): med//mi'i seng ge'i chos nyid dang //dpa' bo'i stobs la rab tu gnas//lus dang mtshan nyid mi 'dra ste//snang ba'i sems dang mnyam pa'i sku//ji ltar 'jig rten de bzhin sku//ji bzhin skur yang rgyal ba ste//
117b4(phal chen): sku'i rang bzhin shes pa ni//sangs rgyas chags pa med ces bya//sangs rgyas kun tu gzigs pa ste//chos rnams thams cad rab tu gzigs//sangs rgyas chos dang byang chub rnams//gang la'ang
117b5(phal chen): dmigs pa med pa yin//sangs rgyas la ni gshegs pa med//sangs rgyas gang na'ang mi bzhugs te//yang dag sangs rgyas log pa med//de bzhin gshegs la 'o na mi mnga'//de nas byang chub
117b6(phal chen): sems dpa' dang la gyi rgyal mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis phyogs bcur rnam par blta te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//sangs rgyas thams cad kun tu khyab//zhing gcig la ni mi gnas
117b7(phal chen): te//thams cad du ni sangs rgyas gshegs//zhing rnams kun tu rab tu snang //sangs rgyas sku ni thams cadu//byin gyi rlabs dag rab tu 'byung //'tshang rgya chos kyi 'khor lo dang //bsam ba bzhin du mya ngan 'da'//
117b8(phal chen): sangs rgyas la ni su zhig sems//ci phyir sangs rgyas bsam mi khyab//yang dag sangs rgyasu yis mthong //rgyal ba sus kyang mthong bar 'gyur//ji ltar chos rnams chos nyid dang //ji ltar sangs rgyas spyod yul
118a1(phal chen): @#//du//gang dag chos rnams gcig tsam yang //de bzhin nyid la mi 'jugo/'di ni 'jig rten 'di rgyal zhes//'jig rten dag na rnam rtog yod//gang gis ngo bo nyid shes na//'jig rten med de rgyal
118a2(phal chen): ba'ang med//'jig rten dad par bya ba'i phyir//mgon po dag ni mngon sum ston//de dag gi ni lta ba 'di//shin tu dmigs par mi rungo//gang gis chags pa rnam bzlog cing //chags pa med la dga' byed pa//chags
118a3(phal chen): pa thams cad rnam bzlog nas//chags med chos nyid rtogs par 'gyur//sangs rgyas zhes brjod gang yin dang //gang gi rnam 'phrul snang gyur pa'i//chos ni dus gsum thams cad du//rnam pa kun tu mi rnyed//
118a4(phal chen): gang gis chos nyid 'di shes shing //gang gis 'di bzhin yid ches na//thams cad mkhyen cing kun gzigs pa'i//de bzhin gshegs par myur du 'gyur//sangs rgyas thams cad rnam 'phrul ba//'di dag
118a5(phal chen): thams cad tshig gis bstan//yang dag sangs rgyas tshig lam med//tshig gi lam gyis rab tu brjod//de nas byang chub sems dpa' chos kyi mtshan gyis//sangs rgyas kyi byin gyi rlabs kyis//
118a6(phal chen): phyogs bcur rnam par bltas te/de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag smraso//gang gis rnam par 'phrul snang ba'i//sangs rgyas mthong ba med pa bas//'jig rten khams rnams thams cad kyi//sdug bsngal phung po
118a7(phal chen): blangs kyang blang //'gro ba gang gis byang chub tu//sems ni nam yang ma bskyed pa//de dag gnas 'di thos gyur na//sangs rgyas byang chub 'grub par 'gyur//gang gis bsam pa gcig gis kyang //'di la mos
118a8(phal chen): byed mkhas pa ni//mi'i mchog tu mi 'gyur zhes//des ni the tsom mi bya'o//de'i rnam par 'phrul pa ni//bskal pa bye ba'i bar du dkon//su zhig 'di la mos byed pa//de dag bla na med par 'gyur//brjod du med
118b1(phal chen): pa'i sangs rgyas rnams//skad cig skad cig bsnyen bkur kyang //'di'i tshul la ma gnas na//de dag bsnyen bkur byas ma yin//gang la sangs rgyas brjod gyur pa'i//chos 'di gang gis thos gyur pa//des ni mi bde kun 
118b2(phal chen): gyis kyang //spyad pa rnams ni blangs bar bya//ji tsam chos kyi dbyings rnams dang //dus gsum gyi ni mgon po yi//sangs rgyas rnams ni rten pa yi//ye shes chen po gcig thos nas//phyi mtha'i bskal ba bye ba rnams//
118b3(phal chen): zhing rnams re rer zad byas shing //'di'i tshul ni brtson byed na//de la byang chub dkon ba med//sems can rnams kyi sngon mtha'i//'khor bar mi rnams gnas gyur kyang //des ni sangs rgyas rab phyung pa//chos nyid
118b4(phal chen): 'di ni shes ma gyur//chos rnams zhig par byas pa med//chos ni 'jig par mi 'gyur te//kun tu snang las rnam 'phrul ba//'jig rten de dag thams cad bstan//shin tu rgyas pa chen po'i mdo//
118b5(phal chen): sangs rgyas phal po che zhes bya ba las/dga' ldan gyi gnasu byang chub sems dpa' 'dus pa'i tshigasu bcad pa bstan pa'i le'u ste/nyi shu dgu pa'o////de nas byang chub
118b6(phal chen): sems dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mtshan de'i tshe/sangs rgyas kyi mthus tinge 'dzin ye shes snang ba zhes bya ba la snyoms par zhugs te/byang chub sems dpa' rdo rje'i rgyal mchan/byang chub sems dpa'i tinge 'dzin ye shes
118b7(phal chen): snang ba zhes bya ba de la snyoms par zhugs ma thag tu/de'i tshe na phyogs bcu'i phyogs re rer 'jig rten gyi khams sangs rgyas kyi zhing stong gi rdul shin tu phra ba snyed 'das pa'i pha rol nas/de bzhin gshegs pa

218b3(phal chen): pa'i yon tan thams cad dang ldan ba/dga' bar byed pa yidu 'ong ba'i rim gros rtse bar byed pa/bkur sti'i tshul la mkhas pa/rol mo brdung ba'i sgra yid 'ong ba shin tu phyung ba dang /rol mo dkrol ba dang glu'i sgra
218b4(phal chen): mi 'thun ba med pa dang /gar gyis nye bar spyod pa dang ldan pa/bud med cang shes kyi tshogs kyis kun nas bskor ba bzhin sdu gu dang cha byad sdu gu'i bu mo rin po che dang ldan ba/phreng ba dang spos dang rgyan gyi
218b5(phal chen): rim gror byed pa/bzhad gad dang /rol mos dga' bar rtse zhing bkur sti rnam pa sna tshogs yidu 'ong ba ston pa'i bud med rin po che 'grogs pa ngag nyan cing 'thun bar 'dzin pa/des pa'i 
218b6(phal chen): tshig dga' bar byed cing rim gror byed pa/rgyan bzang po rab tu mang bas bstan yidu 'ong bar brgyan pas shin tu rnam par dag pa/rol mo brdung ba dang dkrol ba bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong dang ldan ba/byang
